USER # Employee Leave Management (ELM)
USER 
USER Synchronized Problem Statement and Controlled Context
USER 
USER Status: FINAL SYNCHRONIZED BASELINE | Version 1.4
USER 
USER > Revision basis
USER This final synchronized baseline incorporates the 18 domain adjudications made after the Phase-2 materials, the coherent-CFR / atomic-AFR requirements arrangement, and the finalized UC/WF formalism. The historical SRS-EL-001…107 set remains the immutable as-administered student baseline and is preserved through the companion crosswalk.
USER 
USER ## 1. Context
USER 
USER Fusion is the institute ERP used to provide administrative, academic, student, and related services. Users may hold one or more roles, and access to ELM capabilities is controlled through role-based permissions.
USER 
USER ELM supports leave-related activities for institute employees. Employees are Faculty or Staff, each with a designation and an organizational unit such as a Department, Section, or Office. Each organizational unit has a Unit Head. Depending on leave type and hierarchy, the Unit Head either gives the final decision or reviews and records Recommended / Not Recommended before the request moves through the applicable higher-sanction path.
USER 
USER ## 2. Leave categories and Station Leave
USER 
USER ELM supports CL, RH, SCL, EL, COL, and VL. Station Leave permission/details are captured within the same leave application when the employee will be outside Jabalpur; Station Leave is not a separate leave-request process.
USER 
USER ## 3. Entitlement, applicability, and carry-forward
USER 
USER CL: 8 days/year for each employee; first-half and second-half CL are supported; an approved half day consumes 0.5 CL day; unused CL does not carry forward.
USER 
USER RH: 2 days/year for each employee; an RH request must correspond to an applicable Restricted Holiday in the currently effective published holiday/RH calendar; unused RH does not carry forward.
USER 
USER SCL: up to 15 days/year for each employee for a special purpose considered admissible by the applicable Sanctioning Authority; no separate encoded admissibility rule is required in this baseline; unused SCL does not carry forward.
USER 
USER EL: non-faculty employees receive 30 days/year directly; unused EL carries forward.
USER 
USER VL: faculty receive 60 VL associated with vacation periods supplied through the institute Academic Calendar. VL may be availed only during an applicable vacation period. At year end, all unused VL converts to EL at 2 VL : 1 EL, including fractional conversion. VL itself does not carry forward; converted EL is credited to the faculty member’s EL balance, may carry forward, and may later be availed.
USER 
USER COL: 20 days/year in this baseline for each eligible employee; COL is medical/balance-controlled leave, supporting medical evidence may be required where applicable, and unused COL carries forward.
USER 
USER ## 4. Leave-day counting
USER 
USER For EL, COL, and VL, leave is counted continuously across the approved leave interval. Intervening weekends and closed holidays within the interval count as leave days. This continuing-count rule does not apply to CL, SCL, or RH.
USER 
USER ## 5. Applying for leave and validation
USER 
USER The employee submits one leave application containing leave category, requested period, purpose/reason, and other required information. Station Leave details are included in the same application when applicable.
USER 
USER Before a new request is allowed to proceed, ELM validates the employee’s leave-category eligibility, available entitlement/balance, applicable leave-counting and calendar conditions, and impermissible overlap with the employee’s active pending or approved leave. First-half and second-half CL on the same date are not treated as overlapping when they occupy different halves.
USER 
USER Leave-policy parameters do not use a draft/publish/effective-version lifecycle in this baseline. Validation uses the current configured leave-policy parameters. Date and RH validation use the currently effective published holiday/RH calendar.
USER 
USER ## 6. Substitute / responsibility coverage
USER 
USER When responsibility coverage is required, the applicant nominates one substitute. The substitute must be another employee, must not be the applicant, and must not have overlapping pending or approved leave during the responsibility period.
USER 
USER The nominated substitute may accept or decline the assignment. Required substitute consent must be accepted before approval routing continues. If the substitute declines, the applicant may nominate another eligible substitute or withdraw the pending request.
USER 
USER ## 7. Review, sanction, and decision communication
USER 
USER For CL and RH, including associated Station Leave, the request is routed to the applicant’s Unit Head and the Unit Head is the final authority.
USER 
USER For SCL, EL, COL, and VL, the Unit Head reviews the request and records Recommended or Not Recommended. Not Recommended is not a final rejection for these categories. The request then proceeds through the configured hierarchy to the appropriate final Sanctioning Authority.
USER 
USER The processing/sanctioning path is resolved from the applicant, organizational unit, designation/role, leave category, and current hierarchy configuration. Named roles may include Deputy Registrar (Estt.), Dean, Registrar, and Director. An Establishment processing step occurs where configured. The Director’s own leave follows the Director-specific self-sanction rule.
USER 
USER On a final decision, the decision is recorded and the employee is notified. On final approval, the employee’s leave record/account is also updated. Intermediate decisions and state changes need not generate general notifications; the employee can inspect them through leave status/history.
USER 
USER ## 8. Pending request, withdrawal, and approved-leave cancellation
USER 
USER A submitted pending leave request is not modified in place. If a change is required before final approval, the employee may withdraw the pending request and submit a new application if needed.
USER 
USER A pending request may be withdrawn immediately at any time before final approval. Upon successful withdrawal, the withdrawal becomes effective immediately, all remaining approval processing for that request terminates, later approve/reject actions are prevented, and the concerned roles are informed.
USER 
USER After final approval, withdrawal is no longer available. Before the approved leave begins, the employee may request cancellation. Cancellation follows the same applicable hierarchy as the original leave and the approved leave is not edited in place. If cancellation is finally approved, the leave is marked Cancelled and any leave balance already deducted for the approval is restored or adjusted accordingly. If cancellation is rejected, the original approved leave remains unchanged. If revised leave is still required after cancellation, a fresh application is submitted.
USER 
USER Once leave has begun, pre-start cancellation no longer applies; change is handled through the applicable extension or resumption process.
USER 
USER ## 9. Extension
USER 
USER An employee already on ongoing EL, COL, or VL may request additional leave time. The extension is validated using the applicable eligibility, balance, substitute, calendar, and approval conditions; required substitute consent is obtained before approval routing continues; and the extension follows the applicable Unit Head and configured higher-sanction path.
USER 
USER When an extension is approved, only the incremental additional leave period is posted and the original approved leave remains the base. When an extension is rejected, the original approved leave remains unchanged.
USER 
USER ## 10. Resumption
USER 
USER Resumption is supported for EL, COL, and VL. After returning, the employee submits the actual resumption date and required resumption details through ELM for review by the applicable Sanctioning Authority.
USER 
USER When the employee resumes on the approved end date, the Sanctioning Authority reviews the resumption and no adjustment is made to the approved leave period or leave balance. When the employee resumes earlier or later than the approved end date, ELM records the actual leave period after review, adjusts the leave balance accordingly, and synchronizes the relevant leave/attendance records.
USER 
USER ## 11. Inquiry and employee-accessible records
USER 
USER ELM provides employees with access to their current leave balances and leave-account/request history, including request status and recorded final decisions. ELM also provides an employee-accessible printable/exportable representation of a leave application.
USER 
USER ## 12. Administrative functions and calendars
USER 
USER Authorized administrative roles maintain leave-policy parameters, the relevant hierarchy configuration, and leave records where authorized. Policy parameters do not require a draft/publish/effective-version lifecycle; ELM uses the current configured policy and hierarchy values.
USER 
USER Authorized administrative roles create, revise, and publish the holiday/RH calendar. Date/RH validation uses the currently effective published holiday/RH calendar until a replacement is published.
USER 
USER Vacation periods used for VL are supplied through the institute Academic Calendar, which is maintained outside ELM by the appropriate academic module/service and is assumed to be available to ELM.
USER 
USER Authorized Leave Administrators may retrospectively record leave already sanctioned through an authorized paper/offline process where ELM could not be used because of illness, connectivity, server unavailability, or another acceptable reason. The entry captures sanction/reference evidence, is validated for record consistency, and updates the relevant leave balance/history. This is record synchronization, not a second approval workflow.
USER 
USER ## 13. Event-triggered year-end processing
USER 
USER When the configured year-end boundary is reached, ELM applies the defined category outcomes: unused CL, RH, and SCL lapse; unused faculty VL is converted to EL; EL and COL carry forward according to policy. The resulting leave-account transactions are recorded and processing exceptions are reported. The year-end boundary is an event trigger, not an ELM actor.
USER 
USER ## 14. SLA
USER 
USER Actionable workflow tasks use configurable reminder and escalation thresholds. When a task is assigned, the assigned user is notified. If the task is still pending at the reminder threshold, the responsible user is reminded and the action is logged; no reminder is issued if the task is no longer pending. If the task remains pending past the escalation threshold, ELM notifies or escalates to the configured higher/responsible authority and logs the escalation; no escalation is issued if the task is no longer pending.
USER 
USER ## 15. Scope boundary
USER 
USER This ELM baseline limits leave categories to CL, RH, SCL, EL, COL, VL, and Station Leave as defined above. Other leave types and low-level design/implementation details are outside this requirements baseline. The Academic Calendar is an external dependency for VL vacation-period data.
USER 
USER ## Appendix A — Actor, Role, and External Dependency Registry
USER 
USER | ID | Preferred term | Responsibility |
USER |---|---|---|
USER | ACT-01 | Employee | Faculty or Staff member applying for and managing own leave. |
USER | ACT-02 | Substitute / Responsibility Holder | Employee nominated to accept responsibility during the applicant’s absence. |
USER | ACT-03 | Unit Head | Head of Department/Section/Office; final authority for CL/RH or reviewer/recommender for higher-sanction leave. |
USER | ACT-04 | Deputy Registrar (Estt.) / Establishment Processing Role | Performs establishment-level verification/routing/record functions where configured. |
USER | ACT-05 | Dean | Competent authority where applicable. |
USER | ACT-06 | Registrar | Competent authority where applicable. |
USER | ACT-07 | Director | Highest competent authority; Director-specific self-sanction rule applies. |
USER | ACT-08 | Leave Administrator | Maintains authorized ELM configuration/records and records offline-sanctioned leave where authorized. |
USER | EXT-01 | Academic Calendar service/module | External source of published vacation periods used for VL applicability. |
USER | EVT-01 | Year-end boundary | Event trigger for EL-UC-020 year-end leave processing; not an actor. |
USER | EVT-02 | Configured SLA threshold reached | Event trigger for EL-UC-021 SLA threshold processing; not an actor. |
USER 
USER Faculty and Staff are employee categories. Unit Head, Substitute, Leave Administrator, Deputy Registrar (Estt.), Dean, Registrar, and Director are roles/authorities. EXT-01 is an external information dependency rather than an ELM actor performing a leave workflow action. EVT-01 and EVT-02 are event triggers; they initiate event-triggered use cases but are not actors.
USER 
USER ## Appendix B — Controlled Glossary
USER 
USER - **Term:** Meaning
USER - **Employee:** Faculty or Staff member using ELM for own leave activity.
USER - **Applicant:** Employee whose leave request is being processed.
USER - **Unit Head:** Head of the applicant’s Department, Section, or Office.
USER - **Substitute / Responsibility Holder:** One eligible employee nominated to carry responsibility during the applicant’s absence.
USER - **Sanctioning Authority:** Competent authority with final approval/rejection power for the applicable routed request.
USER - **Station Leave:** Permission/details associated with leaving Jabalpur, captured inside the same leave application.
USER - **Withdrawal:** Immediate termination of a pending request before final approval, including termination of remaining approval processing.
USER - **Cancellation:** Pre-start reversal request for an approved leave, processed through the applicable hierarchy.
USER - **Extension:** Request for additional leave time while EL/COL/VL is ongoing.
USER - **Resumption:** Employee declaration of actual return to duty after EL/COL/VL, reviewed by the applicable Sanctioning Authority.
USER - **Early resumption:** Return before the approved end date, followed after review by actual-period/balance adjustment.
USER - **Late resumption:** Return after the approved end date, followed after review by actual-period/balance adjustment.
USER - **Offline-sanctioned leave:** Leave already authorized through a valid paper/offline route and later entered into ELM for synchronization with sanction/reference evidence.
USER - **SLA:** Configurable response-time expectation for actionable workflow tasks, with pending-only reminder and escalation.
USER ```

USER ../elm_specs_md/pilot_kit/chunks/problem_statement.md

USER # Employee Leave Management (ELM)
USER 
USER Synchronized Problem Statement and Controlled Context
USER 
USER Status: FINAL SYNCHRONIZED BASELINE | Version 1.4
USER 
USER > Revision basis
USER This final synchronized baseline incorporates the 18 domain adjudications made after the Phase-2 materials, the coherent-CFR / atomic-AFR requirements arrangement, and the finalized UC/WF formalism. The historical SRS-EL-001…107 set remains the immutable as-administered student baseline and is preserved through the companion crosswalk.
USER 
USER ## 1. Context
USER 
USER Fusion is the institute ERP used to provide administrative, academic, student, and related services. Users may hold one or more roles, and access to ELM capabilities is controlled through role-based permissions.
USER 
USER ELM supports leave-related activities for institute employees. Employees are Faculty or Staff, each with a designation and an organizational unit such as a Department, Section, or Office. Each organizational unit has a Unit Head. Depending on leave type and hierarchy, the Unit Head either gives the final decision or reviews and records Recommended / Not Recommended before the request moves through the applicable higher-sanction path.
USER 
USER ## 2. Leave categories and Station Leave
USER 
USER ELM supports CL, RH, SCL, EL, COL, and VL. Station Leave permission/details are captured within the same leave application when the employee will be outside Jabalpur; Station Leave is not a separate leave-request process.
USER 
USER ## 3. Entitlement, applicability, and carry-forward
USER 
USER CL: 8 days/year for each employee; first-half and second-half CL are supported; an approved half day consumes 0.5 CL day; unused CL does not carry forward.
USER 
USER RH: 2 days/year for each employee; an RH request must correspond to an applicable Restricted Holiday in the currently effective published holiday/RH calendar; unused RH does not carry forward.
USER 
USER SCL: up to 15 days/year for each employee for a special purpose considered admissible by the applicable Sanctioning Authority; no separate encoded admissibility rule is required in this baseline; unused SCL does not carry forward.
USER 
USER EL: non-faculty employees receive 30 days/year directly; unused EL carries forward.
USER 
USER VL: faculty receive 60 VL associated with vacation periods supplied through the institute Academic Calendar. VL may be availed only during an applicable vacation period. At year end, all unused VL converts to EL at 2 VL : 1 EL, including fractional conversion. VL itself does not carry forward; converted EL is credited to the faculty member’s EL balance, may carry forward, and may later be availed.
USER 
USER COL: 20 days/year in this baseline for each eligible employee; COL is medical/balance-controlled leave, supporting medical evidence may be required where applicable, and unused COL carries forward.
USER 
USER ## 4. Leave-day counting
USER 
USER For EL, COL, and VL, leave is counted continuously across the approved leave interval. Intervening weekends and closed holidays within the interval count as leave days. This continuing-count rule does not apply to CL, SCL, or RH.
USER 
USER ## 5. Applying for leave and validation
USER 
USER The employee submits one leave application containing leave category, requested period, purpose/reason, and other required information. Station Leave details are included in the same application when applicable.
USER 
USER Before a new request is allowed to proceed, ELM validates the employee’s leave-category eligibility, available entitlement/balance, applicable leave-counting and calendar conditions, and impermissible overlap with the employee’s active pending or approved leave. First-half and second-half CL on the same date are not treated as overlapping when they occupy different halves.
USER 
USER Leave-policy parameters do not use a draft/publish/effective-version lifecycle in this baseline. Validation uses the current configured leave-policy parameters. Date and RH validation use the currently effective published holiday/RH calendar.
USER 
USER ## 6. Substitute / responsibility coverage
USER 
USER When responsibility coverage is required, the applicant nominates one substitute. The substitute must be another employee, must not be the applicant, and must not have overlapping pending or approved leave during the responsibility period.
USER 
USER The nominated substitute may accept or decline the assignment. Required substitute consent must be accepted before approval routing continues. If the substitute declines, the applicant may nominate another eligible substitute or withdraw the pending request.
USER 
USER ## 7. Review, sanction, and decision communication
USER 
USER For CL and RH, including associated Station Leave, the request is routed to the applicant’s Unit Head and the Unit Head is the final authority.
USER 
USER For SCL, EL, COL, and VL, the Unit Head reviews the request and records Recommended or Not Recommended. Not Recommended is not a final rejection for these categories. The request then proceeds through the configured hierarchy to the appropriate final Sanctioning Authority.
USER 
USER The processing/sanctioning path is resolved from the applicant, organizational unit, designation/role, leave category, and current hierarchy configuration. Named roles may include Deputy Registrar (Estt.), Dean, Registrar, and Director. An Establishment processing step occurs where configured. The Director’s own leave follows the Director-specific self-sanction rule.
USER 
USER On a final decision, the decision is recorded and the employee is notified. On final approval, the employee’s leave record/account is also updated. Intermediate decisions and state changes need not generate general notifications; the employee can inspect them through leave status/history.
USER 
USER ## 8. Pending request, withdrawal, and approved-leave cancellation
USER 
USER A submitted pending leave request is not modified in place. If a change is required before final approval, the employee may withdraw the pending request and submit a new application if needed.
USER 
USER A pending request may be withdrawn immediately at any time before final approval. Upon successful withdrawal, the withdrawal becomes effective immediately, all remaining approval processing for that request terminates, later approve/reject actions are prevented, and the concerned roles are informed.
USER 
USER After final approval, withdrawal is no longer available. Before the approved leave begins, the employee may request cancellation. Cancellation follows the same applicable hierarchy as the original leave and the approved leave is not edited in place. If cancellation is finally approved, the leave is marked Cancelled and any leave balance already deducted for the approval is restored or adjusted accordingly. If cancellation is rejected, the original approved leave remains unchanged. If revised leave is still required after cancellation, a fresh application is submitted.
USER 
USER Once leave has begun, pre-start cancellation no longer applies; change is handled through the applicable extension or resumption process.
USER 
USER ## 9. Extension
USER 
USER An employee already on ongoing EL, COL, or VL may request additional leave time. The extension is validated using the applicable eligibility, balance, substitute, calendar, and approval conditions; required substitute consent is obtained before approval routing continues; and the extension follows the applicable Unit Head and configured higher-sanction path.
USER 
USER When an extension is approved, only the incremental additional leave period is posted and the original approved leave remains the base. When an extension is rejected, the original approved leave remains unchanged.
USER 
USER ## 10. Resumption
USER 
USER Resumption is supported for EL, COL, and VL. After returning, the employee submits the actual resumption date and required resumption details through ELM for review by the applicable Sanctioning Authority.
USER 
USER When the employee resumes on the approved end date, the Sanctioning Authority reviews the resumption and no adjustment is made to the approved leave period or leave balance. When the employee resumes earlier or later than the approved end date, ELM records the actual leave period after review, adjusts the leave balance accordingly, and synchronizes the relevant leave/attendance records.
USER 
USER ## 11. Inquiry and employee-accessible records
USER 
USER ELM provides employees with access to their current leave balances and leave-account/request history, including request status and recorded final decisions. ELM also provides an employee-accessible printable/exportable representation of a leave application.
USER 
USER ## 12. Administrative functions and calendars
USER 
USER Authorized administrative roles maintain leave-policy parameters, the relevant hierarchy configuration, and leave records where authorized. Policy parameters do not require a draft/publish/effective-version lifecycle; ELM uses the current configured policy and hierarchy values.
USER 
USER Authorized administrative roles create, revise, and publish the holiday/RH calendar. Date/RH validation uses the currently effective published holiday/RH calendar until a replacement is published.
USER 
USER Vacation periods used for VL are supplied through the institute Academic Calendar, which is maintained outside ELM by the appropriate academic module/service and is assumed to be available to ELM.
USER 
USER Authorized Leave Administrators may retrospectively record leave already sanctioned through an authorized paper/offline process where ELM could not be used because of illness, connectivity, server unavailability, or another acceptable reason. The entry captures sanction/reference evidence, is validated for record consistency, and updates the relevant leave balance/history. This is record synchronization, not a second approval workflow.
USER 
USER ## 13. Event-triggered year-end processing
USER 
USER When the configured year-end boundary is reached, ELM applies the defined category outcomes: unused CL, RH, and SCL lapse; unused faculty VL is converted to EL; EL and COL carry forward according to policy. The resulting leave-account transactions are recorded and processing exceptions are reported. The year-end boundary is an event trigger, not an ELM actor.
USER 
USER ## 14. SLA
USER 
USER Actionable workflow tasks use configurable reminder and escalation thresholds. When a task is assigned, the assigned user is notified. If the task is still pending at the reminder threshold, the responsible user is reminded and the action is logged; no reminder is issued if the task is no longer pending. If the task remains pending past the escalation threshold, ELM notifies or escalates to the configured higher/responsible authority and logs the escalation; no escalation is issued if the task is no longer pending.
USER 
USER ## 15. Scope boundary
USER 
USER This ELM baseline limits leave categories to CL, RH, SCL, EL, COL, VL, and Station Leave as defined above. Other leave types and low-level design/implementation details are outside this requirements baseline. The Academic Calendar is an external dependency for VL vacation-period data.
USER 
USER ## Appendix A — Actor, Role, and External Dependency Registry
USER 
USER | ID | Preferred term | Responsibility |
USER |---|---|---|
USER | ACT-01 | Employee | Faculty or Staff member applying for and managing own leave. |
USER | ACT-02 | Substitute / Responsibility Holder | Employee nominated to accept responsibility during the applicant’s absence. |
USER | ACT-03 | Unit Head | Head of Department/Section/Office; final authority for CL/RH or reviewer/recommender for higher-sanction leave. |
USER | ACT-04 | Deputy Registrar (Estt.) / Establishment Processing Role | Performs establishment-level verification/routing/record functions where configured. |
USER | ACT-05 | Dean | Competent authority where applicable. |
USER | ACT-06 | Registrar | Competent authority where applicable. |
USER | ACT-07 | Director | Highest competent authority; Director-specific self-sanction rule applies. |
USER | ACT-08 | Leave Administrator | Maintains authorized ELM configuration/records and records offline-sanctioned leave where authorized. |
USER | EXT-01 | Academic Calendar service/module | External source of published vacation periods used for VL applicability. |
USER | EVT-01 | Year-end boundary | Event trigger for EL-UC-020 year-end leave processing; not an actor. |
USER | EVT-02 | Configured SLA threshold reached | Event trigger for EL-UC-021 SLA threshold processing; not an actor. |
USER 
USER Faculty and Staff are employee categories. Unit Head, Substitute, Leave Administrator, Deputy Registrar (Estt.), Dean, Registrar, and Director are roles/authorities. EXT-01 is an external information dependency rather than an ELM actor performing a leave workflow action. EVT-01 and EVT-02 are event triggers; they initiate event-triggered use cases but are not actors.
USER 
USER ## Appendix B — Controlled Glossary
USER 
USER - **Term:** Meaning
USER - **Employee:** Faculty or Staff member using ELM for own leave activity.
USER - **Applicant:** Employee whose leave request is being processed.
USER - **Unit Head:** Head of the applicant’s Department, Section, or Office.
USER - **Substitute / Responsibility Holder:** One eligible employee nominated to carry responsibility during the applicant’s absence.
USER - **Sanctioning Authority:** Competent authority with final approval/rejection power for the applicable routed request.
USER - **Station Leave:** Permission/details associated with leaving Jabalpur, captured inside the same leave application.
USER - **Withdrawal:** Immediate termination of a pending request before final approval, including termination of remaining approval processing.
USER - **Cancellation:** Pre-start reversal request for an approved leave, processed through the applicable hierarchy.
USER - **Extension:** Request for additional leave time while EL/COL/VL is ongoing.
USER - **Resumption:** Employee declaration of actual return to duty after EL/COL/VL, reviewed by the applicable Sanctioning Authority.
USER - **Early resumption:** Return before the approved end date, followed after review by actual-period/balance adjustment.
USER - **Late resumption:** Return after the approved end date, followed after review by actual-period/balance adjustment.
USER - **Offline-sanctioned leave:** Leave already authorized through a valid paper/offline route and later entered into ELM for synchronization with sanction/reference evidence.
USER - **SLA:** Configurable response-time expectation for actionable workflow tasks, with pending-only reminder and escalation.
USER ```

USER core/api/csrf.py
USER ```
USER """CSRF for cookie-borne credentials, checked in the authentication classes —
USER the one layer that knows how the credential arrived.
USER 
USER The token is HMAC(SECRET_KEY, session): stored nowhere, bound to one session.
USER Backs up SameSite=Lax for a same-site subdomain or a browser that ignores it.
USER """
USER from __future__ import annotations
USER 
USER import hashlib
USER import hmac
USER 
USER from django.conf import settings
USER from rest_framework.exceptions import PermissionDenied
USER 
USER HEADER = "HTTP_X_CSRF_TOKEN"
USER SAFE_METHODS = frozenset({"GET", "HEAD", "OPTIONS", "TRACE"})
USER 
USER 
USER def token_for(session_value: str) -> str:
USER     """The token that pairs with this session. Derived, never stored."""
USER     return hmac.new(settings.SECRET_KEY.encode(),
USER                     f"csrf:{session_value}".encode(),
USER                     hashlib.sha256).hexdigest()
USER 
USER 
USER def require(request, session_value: str) -> None:
USER     """Raise unless an unsafe cookie-authenticated request carries it."""
USER     if request.method in SAFE_METHODS:
USER         return
USER     sent = request.META.get(HEADER, "")
USER     if not hmac.compare_digest(sent, token_for(session_value)):
USER         raise PermissionDenied(
USER             "Missing or invalid CSRF token. Send the `csrf_token` from your "
USER             "session payload in the X-CSRF-Token header.")
USER ```
USER 
USER modules/directory/models.py
USER ```
USER """A local projection of IAM's directory.
USER 
USER Kept for joins (you cannot ORDER BY a field that lives behind an HTTP call)
USER and for resilience while IAM is briefly down. A cache with a table, never a
USER source of truth — nothing here is edited by hand.
USER """
USER from django.db import models
USER 
USER from core.db.mixins import TimeStampedModel
USER 
USER 
USER class UserRef(TimeStampedModel):
USER     KIND_CHOICES = [
USER         ("student", "Student"), ("faculty", "Faculty"),
USER         ("staff", "Staff"), ("operator", "Operator"),
USER     ]
USER 
USER     user_id = models.IntegerField(primary_key=True)     # IAM's id
USER     username = models.CharField(max_length=64, db_index=True)
USER     display_name = models.CharField(max_length=150)
USER     kind = models.CharField(max_length=16, choices=KIND_CHOICES, db_index=True)
USER     email = models.EmailField(blank=True)
USER     department = models.CharField(max_length=80, blank=True)
USER     programme = models.CharField(max_length=40, blank=True)
USER     discipline = models.CharField(max_length=40, blank=True, db_index=True)
USER     batch_year = models.PositiveSmallIntegerField(null=True, blank=True)
USER     is_active = models.BooleanField(default=True)
USER     #: The ERP portal owns these; a student keeps one resume, not one per module.
USER     resume_link = models.CharField(max_length=500, blank=True)
USER     profile_completed = models.BooleanField(default=False)
USER     synced_at = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)
USER 
USER     class Meta:
USER         db_table = "directory_user_ref"
USER         indexes = [
USER             models.Index(fields=["kind", "discipline"], name="userref_kind_disc_idx"),
USER             models.Index(fields=["batch_year"], name="userref_batch_idx"),
USER         ]
USER 
USER     def __str__(self) -> str:
USER         return f"{self.display_name} <{self.username}>"
USER ```

USER fusion_auth/principal.py
USER ```
USER """The authenticated actor.
USER 
USER Called `principal`, not `user`, deliberately: there is no Django user behind
USER it. It is a value object built from what IAM said, and it is what every
USER permission check reads.
USER """
USER from __future__ import annotations
USER 
USER from dataclasses import dataclass
USER 
USER from fusion_auth.client import IamSession
USER 
USER 
USER @dataclass(frozen=True)
USER class Principal:
USER     user_id: int
USER     username: str
USER     display_name: str
USER     kind: str
USER     active_role: str | None
USER     roles: tuple[str, ...]
USER     permissions: frozenset[str]
USER     modules: tuple[str, ...]
USER     #: Built by the IAM so one sidebar can span every Fusion app.
USER     navigation: tuple[dict, ...] = ()
USER     email: str = ""
USER 
USER     # DRF pokes at these on request.user
USER     is_authenticated: bool = True
USER     is_anonymous: bool = False
USER 
USER     @classmethod
USER     def from_session(cls, s: IamSession) -> Principal:
USER         return cls(
USER             user_id=s.user_id, username=s.username, display_name=s.display_name,
USER             kind=s.kind, active_role=s.active_role, roles=s.roles,
USER             permissions=s.permissions, modules=s.modules, email=s.email,
USER             navigation=s.navigation,
USER         )
USER 
USER     def has_permission(self, code: str) -> bool:
USER         return code in self.permissions
USER 
USER     def has_any_permission(self, *codes: str) -> bool:
USER         return any(c in self.permissions for c in codes)
USER 
USER     def has_module(self, code: str) -> bool:
USER         return code in self.modules
USER 
USER     def __str__(self) -> str:
USER         return f"{self.username}({self.user_id})"
USER ```
USER 
USER core/api/exceptions.py
USER ```
USER """One error envelope for the whole platform:
USER 
USER     {"error": {"code": ..., "message": ..., "details": [...], "request_id": ...}}
USER 
USER `request_id` is always present, so a user reads it off a toast and support
USER greps one id across the logs.
USER """
USER from rest_framework import status
USER from rest_framework.response import Response
USER from rest_framework.views import exception_handler as drf_handler
USER 
USER from core.observability.middleware import get_request_id
USER 
USER 
USER class DomainError(Exception):
USER     """Base for errors raised by domain/ and services/.
USER 
USER     Deliberately not a DRF exception — a service must stay callable from a
USER     Celery task or a management command, where HTTP status is noise.
USER     """
USER 
USER     code = "domain_error"
USER     status_code = status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY
USER     message = "The request could not be processed."
USER 
USER     def __init__(self, message=None, *, code=None, details=None):
USER         super().__init__(message or self.message)
USER         self.message = message or self.message
USER         self.code = code or self.code
USER         self.details = details or []
USER 
USER 
USER class BadRequestError(DomainError):
USER     """The request itself is malformed — a non-numeric id, an unparseable
USER     filter. Distinct from ConflictError's 409, which means the request was
USER     well-formed and the resource refused it."""
USER 
USER     code = "bad_request"
USER     status_code = status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST
USER     message = "The request could not be understood."
USER 
USER 
USER class NotFoundError(DomainError):
USER     code = "not_found"
USER     status_code = status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND
USER     message = "Not found."
USER 
USER 
USER class ConflictError(DomainError):
USER     code = "conflict"
USER     status_code = status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT
USER     message = "The resource is not in a state that allows this."
USER 
USER 
USER class AuthenticationFailedError(DomainError):
USER     """A credential was rejected: 401 "we do not know who you are", as opposed
USER     to PermissionDeniedError's 403 "we know, and no". Collapsing the two makes
USER     an auth outage indistinguishable from a misconfigured grant in the logs."""
USER 
USER     code = "invalid_credentials"
USER     status_code = status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED
USER     message = "Incorrect username or password."
USER 
USER 
USER class PermissionDeniedError(DomainError):
USER     code = "permission_denied"
USER     status_code = status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN
USER     message = "You do not have permission to perform this action."
USER 
USER 
USER def _envelope(code, message, details, http_status):
USER     return Response(
USER         {
USER             "error": {
USER                 "code": code,
USER                 "message": message,
USER                 "details": details or [],
USER                 "request_id": get_request_id(),
USER             }
USER         },
USER         status=http_status,
USER     )
USER 
USER 
USER def exception_handler(exc, context):
USER     if isinstance(exc, DomainError):
USER         return _envelope(exc.code, exc.message, exc.details, exc.status_code)
USER 
USER     response = drf_handler(exc, context)
USER     if response is None:
USER         return None                      # let Django produce the 500
USER 
USER     detail = response.data
USER     details = []
USER     if isinstance(detail, dict) and "detail" not in detail:
USER         details = [
USER             {"field": f, "message": m[0] if isinstance(m, list) else str(m)}
USER             for f, m in detail.items()
USER         ]
USER         message = "The submitted data was not valid."
USER         code = "validation_error"
USER     else:
USER         message = str(detail.get("detail")) if isinstance(detail, dict) else str(detail)
USER         code = getattr(exc, "default_code", "error")
USER     return _envelope(code, message, details, response.status_code)
USER ```
USER 
USER client/src/modules/placement/api/hooks.ts
USER ```
USER /** Data access for the placement module.
USER  *
USER  *  Every mutation invalidates the keys it can affect; no manual cache surgery.
USER  *  Getting that subtly wrong shows up as an enabled "Apply" button on a
USER  *  posting the student already applied to.
USER  */
USER import {
USER   useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient, type UseMutationOptions,
USER } from "@tanstack/react-query";
USER 
USER import { filenameFromDisposition, saveBlob } from "../../../lib/download";
USER import { http, readBlobError } from "../../../lib/http";
USER import type {
USER   Announcement, Applicant, Application, Company, EligibilityVerdict,
USER   InterviewRound, Offer, Page, Posting, ProfileDocument, StaffStats,
USER   StudentStats,
USER } from "./types";
USER 
USER const KEY = "placement";
USER 
USER export const keys = {
USER   postings: (f?: unknown) => [KEY, "postings", f] as const,
USER   posting: (id: number) => [KEY, "posting", id] as const,
USER   eligibility: (id: number) => [KEY, "eligibility", id] as const,
USER   applications: (f?: unknown) => [KEY, "applications", f] as const,
USER   offers: () => [KEY, "offers"] as const,
USER   resume: () => [KEY, "resume"] as const,
USER   companies: () => [KEY, "companies"] as const,
USER   rounds: (postingId?: number) => [KEY, "rounds", postingId] as const,
USER   announcements: () => [KEY, "announcements"] as const,
USER   stats: (season?: string) => [KEY, "stats", season] as const,
USER   seasons: () => [KEY, "seasons"] as const,
USER   registrations: () => [KEY, "registrations"] as const,
USER   records: () => [KEY, "records"] as const,
USER   clearance: () => [KEY, "clearance"] as const,
USER   outstanding: (season?: string) => [KEY, "outstanding", season] as const,
USER   history: (id: number) => [KEY, "history", id] as const,
USER   myConduct: () => [KEY, "my-conduct"] as const,
USER   registrationTerms: (season?: string) =>
USER     [KEY, "registration-terms", season] as const,
USER };
USER 
USER /** Coarse on purpose — these pages are small, and correctness beats shaving
USER  *  a refetch. */
USER function invalidateAll(qc: ReturnType<typeof useQueryClient>) {
USER   qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [KEY] });
USER }
USER 
USER // -- Postings ------------------------------------------------------------------
USER export function usePostings(filters?: { placement_year?: string; kind?: string }) {
USER   return useQuery({
USER     queryKey: keys.postings(filters),
USER     queryFn: async () =>
USER       (await http.get<Page<Posting>>("/placement/postings", { params: filters }))
USER         .data,
USER     staleTime: 30_000,
USER   });
USER }
USER 
USER export function usePosting(id: number | undefined) {
USER   return useQuery({
USER     queryKey: keys.posting(id!),
USER     queryFn: async () =>
USER       (await http.get<Posting>(`/placement/postings/${id}`)).data,
USER     enabled: id != null,
USER   });
USER }
USER 
USER /** The "why can't I apply?" call, fetched when a posting is opened.
USER  *
USER  *  Deliberately not folded into the list query — it reaches the IAM for a
USER  *  declared CPI, so per-row would be fifty round trips nobody asked for. */
USER export function useEligibility(postingId: number | undefined) {
USER   return useQuery({
USER     queryKey: keys.eligibility(postingId!),
USER     queryFn: async () =>
USER       (await http.get<EligibilityVerdict>(
USER         `/placement/postings/${postingId}/eligibility`)).data,
USER     enabled: postingId != null,
USER     retry: false,     // a 503 from the IAM should surface, not be retried away
USER     staleTime: 60_000,
USER   });
USER }
USER

USER   const qc = useQueryClient();

USER     mutationFn: async (body: Record<string, unknown>) =>
USER       (await http.post<Posting>("/placement/postings", body)).data,
USER     onSuccess: () => invalidateAll(qc),
USER   });
USER }
USER

USER   });

USER   options?: Pick<UseMutationOptions<Application, unknown,
USER     { id: number; to_status: string; reason?: string }>, "onSuccess" | "onError">,
USER ) {

USER     mutationFn: async ({ id, to_status, reason }:
USER       { id: number; to_status: string; reason?: string }) =>
USER       (await http.post<Application>(
USER         `/placement/applications/${id}/transition`,
USER         { to_status, reason: reason ?? "" })).data,

USER     onError: options?.onError,
USER   });

USER export type ExportKind = "applications" | "placements";
USER 
USER const EXPORT_PATHS: Record<ExportKind, string> = {
USER   applications: "/placement/exports/applications.csv",
USER   placements: "/placement/exports/placements.csv",
USER };
USER 
USER /** Exports are fetched, not navigated to. A link would leave the SPA, so a
USER  *  refused or throttled export lands the officer on a raw server error page;
USER  *  buffering a capped file is the cost of reporting it as a toast instead. */
USER export function useCsvExport() {
USER   return useMutation({
USER     mutationFn: async (kind: ExportKind) => {
USER       try {
USER         const res = await http.get(EXPORT_PATHS[kind], { responseType: "blob" });
USER         saveBlob(
USER           res.data as Blob,
USER           filenameFromDisposition(
USER             res.headers["content-disposition"], `${kind}.csv`),
USER         );
USER       } catch (e) {
USER         throw await readBlobError(e);
USER       }
USER     },
USER   });
USER }
USER

USER ../elm_specs_md/pilot_kit/chunks/problem_statement.md
USER ```
USER # Employee Leave Management (ELM)
USER 
USER Synchronized Problem Statement and Controlled Context
USER 
USER Status: FINAL SYNCHRONIZED BASELINE | Version 1.4
USER 
USER > Revision basis
USER This final synchronized baseline incorporates the 18 domain adjudications made after the Phase-2 materials, the coherent-CFR / atomic-AFR requirements arrangement, and the finalized UC/WF formalism. The historical SRS-EL-001…107 set remains the immutable as-administered student baseline and is preserved through the companion crosswalk.
USER 
USER ## 1. Context
USER 
USER Fusion is the institute ERP used to provide administrative, academic, student, and related services. Users may hold one or more roles, and access to ELM capabilities is controlled through role-based permissions.
USER 
USER ELM supports leave-related activities for institute employees. Employees are Faculty or Staff, each with a designation and an organizational unit such as a Department, Section, or Office. Each organizational unit has a Unit Head. Depending on leave type and hierarchy, the Unit Head either gives the final decision or reviews and records Recommended / Not Recommended before the request moves through the applicable higher-sanction path.
USER 
USER ## 2. Leave categories and Station Leave
USER 
USER ELM supports CL, RH, SCL, EL, COL, and VL. Station Leave permission/details are captured within the same leave application when the employee will be outside Jabalpur; Station Leave is not a separate leave-request process.
USER 
USER ## 3. Entitlement, applicability, and carry-forward
USER 
USER CL: 8 days/year for each employee; first-half and second-half CL are supported; an approved half day consumes 0.5 CL day; unused CL does not carry forward.
USER 
USER RH: 2 days/year for each employee; an RH request must correspond to an applicable Restricted Holiday in the currently effective published holiday/RH calendar; unused RH does not carry forward.
USER 
USER SCL: up to 15 days/year for each employee for a special purpose considered admissible by the applicable Sanctioning Authority; no separate encoded admissibility rule is required in this baseline; unused SCL does not carry forward.
USER 
USER EL: non-faculty employees receive 30 days/year directly; unused EL carries forward.
USER 
USER VL: faculty receive 60 VL associated with vacation periods supplied through the institute Academic Calendar. VL may be availed only during an applicable vacation period. At year end, all unused VL converts to EL at 2 VL : 1 EL, including fractional conversion. VL itself does not carry forward; converted EL is credited to the faculty member’s EL balance, may carry forward, and may later be availed.
USER 
USER COL: 20 days/year in this baseline for each eligible employee; COL is medical/balance-controlled leave, supporting medical evidence may be required where applicable, and unused COL carries forward.
USER 
USER ## 4. Leave-day counting
USER 
USER For EL, COL, and VL, leave is counted continuously across the approved leave interval. Intervening weekends and closed holidays within the interval count as leave days. This continuing-count rule does not apply to CL, SCL, or RH.
USER 
USER ## 5. Applying for leave and validation
USER 
USER The employee submits one leave application containing leave category, requested period, purpose/reason, and other required information. Station Leave details are included in the same application when applicable.
USER 
USER Before a new request is allowed to proceed, ELM validates the employee’s leave-category eligibility, available entitlement/balance, applicable leave-counting and calendar conditions, and impermissible overlap with the employee’s active pending or approved leave. First-half and second-half CL on the same date are not treated as overlapping when they occupy different halves.
USER 
USER Leave-policy parameters do not use a draft/publish/effective-version lifecycle in this baseline. Validation uses the current configured leave-policy parameters. Date and RH validation use the currently effective published holiday/RH calendar.
USER 
USER ## 6. Substitute / responsibility coverage
USER 
USER When responsibility coverage is required, the applicant nominates one substitute. The substitute must be another employee, must not be the applicant, and must not have overlapping pending or approved leave during the responsibility period.
USER 
USER The nominated substitute may accept or decline the assignment. Required substitute consent must be accepted before approval routing continues. If the substitute declines, the applicant may nominate another eligible substitute or withdraw the pending request.
USER 
USER ## 7. Review, sanction, and decision communication
USER 
USER For CL and RH, including associated Station Leave, the request is routed to the applicant’s Unit Head and the Unit Head is the final authority.
USER 
USER For SCL, EL, COL, and VL, the Unit Head reviews the request and records Recommended or Not Recommended. Not Recommended is not a final rejection for these categories. The request then proceeds through the configured hierarchy to the appropriate final Sanctioning Authority.
USER 
USER The processing/sanctioning path is resolved from the applicant, organizational unit, designation/role, leave category, and current hierarchy configuration. Named roles may include Deputy Registrar (Estt.), Dean, Registrar, and Director. An Establishment processing step occurs where configured. The Director’s own leave follows the Director-specific self-sanction rule.
USER 
USER On a final decision, the decision is recorded and the employee is notified. On final approval, the employee’s leave record/account is also updated. Intermediate decisions and state changes need not generate general notifications; the employee can inspect them through leave status/history.
USER 
USER ## 8. Pending request, withdrawal, and approved-leave cancellation
USER 
USER A submitted pending leave request is not modified in place. If a change is required before final approval, the employee may withdraw the pending request and submit a new application if needed.
USER 
USER A pending request may be withdrawn immediately at any time before final approval. Upon successful withdrawal, the withdrawal becomes effective immediately, all remaining approval processing for that request terminates, later approve/reject actions are prevented, and the concerned roles are informed.
USER 
USER After final approval, withdrawal is no longer available. Before the approved leave begins, the employee may request cancellation. Cancellation follows the same applicable hierarchy as the original leave and the approved leave is not edited in place. If cancellation is finally approved, the leave is marked Cancelled and any leave balance already deducted for the approval is restored or adjusted accordingly. If cancellation is rejected, the original approved leave remains unchanged. If revised leave is still required after cancellation, a fresh application is submitted.
USER 
USER Once leave has begun, pre-start cancellation no longer applies; change is handled through the applicable extension or resumption process.
USER 
USER ## 9. Extension
USER 
USER An employee already on ongoing EL, COL, or VL may request additional leave time. The extension is validated using the applicable eligibility, balance, substitute, calendar, and approval conditions; required substitute consent is obtained before approval routing continues; and the extension follows the applicable Unit Head and configured higher-sanction path.
USER 
USER When an extension is approved, only the incremental additional leave period is posted and the original approved leave remains the base. When an extension is rejected, the original approved leave remains unchanged.
USER 
USER ## 10. Resumption
USER 
USER Resumption is supported for EL, COL, and VL. After returning, the employee submits the actual resumption date and required resumption details through ELM for review by the applicable Sanctioning Authority.
USER 
USER When the employee resumes on the approved end date, the Sanctioning Authority reviews the resumption and no adjustment is made to the approved leave period or leave balance. When the employee resumes earlier or later than the approved end date, ELM records the actual leave period after review, adjusts the leave balance accordingly, and synchronizes the relevant leave/attendance records.
USER 
USER ## 11. Inquiry and employee-accessible records
USER 
USER ELM provides employees with access to their current leave balances and leave-account/request history, including request status and recorded final decisions. ELM also provides an employee-accessible printable/exportable representation of a leave application.
USER 
USER ## 12. Administrative functions and calendars
USER 
USER Authorized administrative roles maintain leave-policy parameters, the relevant hierarchy configuration, and leave records where authorized. Policy parameters do not require a draft/publish/effective-version lifecycle; ELM uses the current configured policy and hierarchy values.
USER 
USER Authorized administrative roles create, revise, and publish the holiday/RH calendar. Date/RH validation uses the currently effective published holiday/RH calendar until a replacement is published.
USER 
USER Vacation periods used for VL are supplied through the institute Academic Calendar, which is maintained outside ELM by the appropriate academic module/service and is assumed to be available to ELM.
USER 
USER Authorized Leave Administrators may retrospectively record leave already sanctioned through an authorized paper/offline process where ELM could not be used because of illness, connectivity, server unavailability, or another acceptable reason. The entry captures sanction/reference evidence, is validated for record consistency, and updates the relevant leave balance/history. This is record synchronization, not a second approval workflow.
USER 
USER ## 13. Event-triggered year-end processing
USER 
USER When the configured year-end boundary is reached, ELM applies the defined category outcomes: unused CL, RH, and SCL lapse; unused faculty VL is converted to EL; EL and COL carry forward according to policy. The resulting leave-account transactions are recorded and processing exceptions are reported. The year-end boundary is an event trigger, not an ELM actor.
USER 
USER ## 14. SLA
USER 
USER Actionable workflow tasks use configurable reminder and escalation thresholds. When a task is assigned, the assigned user is notified. If the task is still pending at the reminder threshold, the responsible user is reminded and the action is logged; no reminder is issued if the task is no longer pending. If the task remains pending past the escalation threshold, ELM notifies or escalates to the configured higher/responsible authority and logs the escalation; no escalation is issued if the task is no longer pending.
USER 
USER ## 15. Scope boundary
USER 
USER This ELM baseline limits leave categories to CL, RH, SCL, EL, COL, VL, and Station Leave as defined above. Other leave types and low-level design/implementation details are outside this requirements baseline. The Academic Calendar is an external dependency for VL vacation-period data.
USER 
USER ## Appendix A — Actor, Role, and External Dependency Registry
USER 
USER | ID | Preferred term | Responsibility |
USER |---|---|---|
USER | ACT-01 | Employee | Faculty or Staff member applying for and managing own leave. |
USER | ACT-02 | Substitute / Responsibility Holder | Employee nominated to accept responsibility during the applicant’s absence. |
USER | ACT-03 | Unit Head | Head of Department/Section/Office; final authority for CL/RH or reviewer/recommender for higher-sanction leave. |
USER | ACT-04 | Deputy Registrar (Estt.) / Establishment Processing Role | Performs establishment-level verification/routing/record functions where configured. |
USER | ACT-05 | Dean | Competent authority where applicable. |
USER | ACT-06 | Registrar | Competent authority where applicable. |
USER | ACT-07 | Director | Highest competent authority; Director-specific self-sanction rule applies. |
USER | ACT-08 | Leave Administrator | Maintains authorized ELM configuration/records and records offline-sanctioned leave where authorized. |
USER | EXT-01 | Academic Calendar service/module | External source of published vacation periods used for VL applicability. |
USER | EVT-01 | Year-end boundary | Event trigger for EL-UC-020 year-end leave processing; not an actor. |
USER | EVT-02 | Configured SLA threshold reached | Event trigger for EL-UC-021 SLA threshold processing; not an actor. |
USER 
USER Faculty and Staff are employee categories. Unit Head, Substitute, Leave Administrator, Deputy Registrar (Estt.), Dean, Registrar, and Director are roles/authorities. EXT-01 is an external information dependency rather than an ELM actor performing a leave workflow action. EVT-01 and EVT-02 are event triggers; they initiate event-triggered use cases but are not actors.
USER 
USER ## Appendix B — Controlled Glossary
USER 
USER - **Term:** Meaning
USER - **Employee:** Faculty or Staff member using ELM for own leave activity.
USER - **Applicant:** Employee whose leave request is being processed.
USER - **Unit Head:** Head of the applicant’s Department, Section, or Office.
USER - **Substitute / Responsibility Holder:** One eligible employee nominated to carry responsibility during the applicant’s absence.
USER - **Sanctioning Authority:** Competent authority with final approval/rejection power for the applicable routed request.
USER - **Station Leave:** Permission/details associated with leaving Jabalpur, captured inside the same leave application.
USER - **Withdrawal:** Immediate termination of a pending request before final approval, including termination of remaining approval processing.
USER - **Cancellation:** Pre-start reversal request for an approved leave, processed through the applicable hierarchy.
USER - **Extension:** Request for additional leave time while EL/COL/VL is ongoing.
USER - **Resumption:** Employee declaration of actual return to duty after EL/COL/VL, reviewed by the applicable Sanctioning Authority.
USER - **Early resumption:** Return before the approved end date, followed after review by actual-period/balance adjustment.
USER - **Late resumption:** Return after the approved end date, followed after review by actual-period/balance adjustment.
USER - **Offline-sanctioned leave:** Leave already authorized through a valid paper/offline route and later entered into ELM for synchronization with sanction/reference evidence.
USER - **SLA:** Configurable response-time expectation for actionable workflow tasks, with pending-only reminder and escalation.
USER ```

USER 
USER     `user_id` is the IAM user id. It is a plain integer and NOT a ForeignKey —

USER # ELM Business Rules — categories, entitlement, counting, overlap, substitute (BR-EL-001..015)
USER 
USER (Unedited excerpt from the supplied ELM specification package.)
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-001 — Supported leave-category eligibility
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Eligibility
USER - **Rule Statement:** A new leave request shall use only CL, RH, SCL, EL, COL, or VL and shall satisfy the category-applicability conditions in the current configured ELM policy.
USER - **Applies To:** New leave validation
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Requested category/application context
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Reject/prevent progression when category applicability is not satisfied.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-002–003
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-001, EL-UC-009
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01; CW-EL-03
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Revised to remove published-policy-version semantics.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-002 — Casual Leave entitlement and lapse
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Entitlement
USER - **Rule Statement:** Each employee has 8 CL days per year; unused CL lapses at year end and does not carry forward.
USER - **Applies To:** CL validation/year-end
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** CL account/request
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Enforce 8-day annual entitlement and no carry-forward.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-004, CFR-EL-028
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-001; EL-UC-020
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Active.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-003 — Restricted Holiday entitlement and applicability
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Entitlement / Calendar
USER - **Rule Statement:** Each employee has 2 RH days per year; an RH request must correspond to an applicable Restricted Holiday in the currently effective published holiday/RH calendar; unused RH lapses at year end.
USER - **Applies To:** RH validation/year-end
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** RH request/account
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Enforce entitlement, calendar applicability, and lapse.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-005, CFR-EL-025, CFR-EL-028
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-001, EL-UC-015; EL-UC-020
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Expanded/confirmed after adjudication.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-004 — Special Casual Leave entitlement and admissibility
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Entitlement / Authority
USER - **Rule Statement:** Each employee may use up to 15 SCL days per calendar year for a special purpose considered admissible by the applicable Sanctioning Authority; no separate encoded admissibility criterion is required; unused SCL lapses at year end.
USER - **Applies To:** SCL validation/decision/year-end
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** SCL request/account
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Enforce annual limit; leave purpose is judged by authority; lapse unused SCL.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-006, CFR-EL-028
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-001, EL-UC-005; EL-UC-020
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Clarified: admissibility is authority judgment, not separate encoded rule.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-005 — Non-faculty Earned Leave credit and carry-forward
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Entitlement
USER - **Rule Statement:** Each non-faculty employee shall receive 30 EL days per year and unused EL shall carry forward according to policy.
USER - **Applies To:** EL account/year-end
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Non-faculty EL
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Credit 30/year and preserve unused EL across year boundary.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-007, CFR-EL-028
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-001; EL-UC-020
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Active.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-006 — Faculty Vacation Leave entitlement and vacation-period applicability
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Entitlement / Calendar
USER - **Rule Statement:** Each faculty member shall have 60 VL associated with vacation periods supplied by the institute Academic Calendar, and VL may be availed only during an applicable vacation period.
USER - **Applies To:** VL validation
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Faculty VL request
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Enforce 60 VL and external Academic Calendar vacation-period applicability.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-008
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-001
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Clarified external vacation-period source.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-007 — VL-to-EL year-end conversion
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Calculation / Year-end
USER - **Rule Statement:** At year end, all unused faculty VL shall convert to EL at 2 VL : 1 EL including fractional conversion, and VL itself shall not carry forward.
USER - **Applies To:** Year-end processing
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Unused faculty VL
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Convert at 2:1 including fractions; leave no unused VL balance as VL.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-008, CFR-EL-028
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-020
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** N/A
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Active, consolidated statement.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-008 — Treatment of EL created by VL conversion
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Account
USER - **Rule Statement:** EL created by VL conversion shall be credited to the faculty member's EL balance, may carry forward, and may subsequently be availed.
USER - **Applies To:** Year-end/account use
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Converted EL
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Credit and retain converted EL as usable EL.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-008, CFR-EL-028
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-020; EL-UC-001
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** N/A
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Active.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-009 — Commuted Leave entitlement and treatment
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Entitlement / Evidence
USER - **Rule Statement:** Eligible employees have 20 COL days/year in this baseline; COL is medical/balance-controlled, supporting medical evidence may be required where applicable, and unused COL carries forward.
USER - **Applies To:** COL validation/year-end
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** COL request/account
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Enforce annual entitlement, balance/evidence conditions, and carry-forward.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-009, CFR-EL-028
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-001; EL-UC-020
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Consolidated.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-010 — Half-day CL treatment
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Calculation
USER - **Rule Statement:** CL shall support first-half and second-half requests; an approved half day consumes 0.5 CL day.
USER - **Applies To:** CL validation/accounting
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Half-day CL
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Recognize halves and deduct 0.5 day when approved.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-004
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-001
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Active.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-011 — Continuous-day counting
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Counting
USER - **Rule Statement:** For EL, COL, and VL, intervening weekends and closed holidays inside the continuous approved interval count as leave days; this rule does not apply to CL, SCL, or RH.
USER - **Applies To:** Leave-day calculation
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** EL/COL/VL and excluded categories
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Apply category-specific counting boundary exactly.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-003, CFR-EL-010
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-001, EL-UC-009
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01; CW-EL-03
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Active.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-012 — Applicant non-overlap with half-day CL exception
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Validation
USER - **Rule Statement:** A new request shall not impermissibly overlap the employee's active pending or approved leave; first-half and second-half CL on the same date are non-overlapping when they occupy different halves.
USER - **Applies To:** New-request validation
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Employee leave intervals
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Block impermissible overlap; permit different CL halves on same date.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-003
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-001
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Confirmed PS gap.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-013 — Station Leave coupling
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Scope / Information
USER - **Rule Statement:** Station Leave permission/details, when applicable, shall be captured within the same leave application and shall not create a separate leave-request process.
USER - **Applies To:** Leave application
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Outside-Jabalpur context
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Keep Station Leave inside associated leave application.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-002
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-001
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Active.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-014 — Substitute eligibility
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Eligibility
USER - **Rule Statement:** When responsibility coverage is required, exactly one substitute is nominated; the substitute must be another employee, must not be the applicant, and must have no overlapping pending or approved leave during the responsibility period.
USER - **Applies To:** Substitute nomination
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Nominated employee and responsibility period
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Reject an ineligible nominee.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-011–012
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-001, EL-UC-002, EL-UC-009, EL-UC-019
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01; CW-EL-03
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Expanded to include not-the-applicant condition.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-015 — Substitute consent gate and decline recovery
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Routing / Eligibility
USER - **Rule Statement:** Required approval routing shall not proceed until the nominated substitute accepts. A decline leaves the consent gate closed; the applicant may nominate another eligible substitute or withdraw the pending request where applicable.
USER - **Applies To:** Substitute response
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Pending request requiring responsibility coverage
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Accept releases routing gate; decline does not.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-012
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-002, EL-UC-019
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01; CW-EL-03
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Expanded with adjudicated recovery path.

USER             ("cancelled", "Cancelled"),

ASSISTANT def calculate_consumed_days(
ASSISTANT     requested_from: date,
ASSISTANT     requested_to: date,
ASSISTANT     category: LeaveCategory,
ASSISTANT ) -> float:
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     Calculate the number of leave days consumed.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     BR-EL-011: For EL, COL, VL, weekends/holidays count as leave days.
ASSISTANT                 For CL, SCL, RH, they do not.
ASSISTANT     BR-EL-010: Half-day CL consumes 0.5 day.
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     # Base calculation: number of days in range (inclusive)
ASSISTANT     delta = requested_to - requested_from
ASSISTANT     base_days = delta.days + 1

ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Apply continuous counting rule (BR-EL-011)
ASSISTANT     if is_continuous_counting(category):
ASSISTANT         # Weekends and holidays count as leave days for EL, COL, VL
ASSISTANT         # Note: In a full implementation, we would check calendar objects here.
ASSISTANT         # For now, we assume the input dates are already adjusted or the logic
ASSISTANT         # handles the specific date range provided.
ASSISTANT         # The rule states "intervening weekends and closed holidays ... count as leave days".
ASSISTANT         # This implies we should NOT subtract them. The base_days calculation above
ASSISTANT         # includes all days. If a specific calendar check is needed, it would be
ASSISTANT         # added here to determine if a day is a weekend/holiday and whether to include/exclude.
ASSISTANT         # Given the rule "count as leave days", we keep all days in the range.
ASSISTANT         pass
ASSISTANT     else:
ASSISTANT         # For CL, SCL, RH, weekends and holidays do NOT count.
ASSISTANT         # This would require a calendar service to filter out non-working days.
ASSISTANT         # Placeholder logic: assume standard working days if calendar not available.
ASSISTANT         # In a real system, this would call a calendar API.
ASSISTANT         pass
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Apply half-day consumption (BR-EL-010)
ASSISTANT     # Note: This function calculates total days. If the request is for multiple half-days,
ASSISTANT     # the caller should multiply by 2 or handle the count separately.
ASSISTANT     # However, if the input represents a single day split into halves, we need to know how many halves.
ASSISTANT     # Since this function takes requested_from/to (dates), it implies full days unless specified otherwise.
ASSISTANT     # Let's assume the caller handles half-day logic by passing specific date ranges or flags.
ASSISTANT     # For now, we return base_days adjusted for continuous counting.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     # To properly support half-days, we might need to know if the request spans a single day with multiple halves.
ASSISTANT     # A common pattern is: requested_from=requested_to (same day) -> 1 full day or 2 half days?
ASSISTANT     # The rule says "an approved half day consumes 0.5 CL day".
ASSISTANT     # Let's assume the API passes the number of half-days separately or we infer from context.
ASSISTANT     # For this pure policy module, we will return the count of days as per continuous counting.
ASSISTANT     # The actual deduction logic (0.5 vs 1.0) is likely handled in the service layer based on approval details.
ASSISTANT     # However, to be complete: if it's a half-day request, we need to know it's half.
ASSISTANT     # Let's assume `requested_from` and `requested_to` define the interval.
ASSISTANT     # If the interval is 1 day, is it 1 full or 2 halves? Usually 1 full unless specified.
ASSISTANT     # We will return the count of days in the interval. The service layer will multiply by 0.5 if approved as half-day.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     return base_days

USER from django.db import models
USER from core.db.mixins import TimeStampedModel, UserScopedModel
USER 
USER 
USER class LeaveCategory(models.TextChoices):
USER     CL = "CL", "Casual Leave"
USER     RH = "RH", "Restricted Holiday"
USER     SCL = "SCL", "Special Casual Leave"
USER     EL = "EL", "Earned Leave"
USER     COL = "COL", "Compensatory / Balance Controlled Leave"
USER     VL = "VL", "Vacation Leave"
USER 
USER 
USER class LeaveApplication(UserScopedModel):
USER     """A leave application submitted by an employee."""
USER 
USER     category = models.CharField(
USER         max_length=2,
USER         choices=LeaveCategory.choices,
USER         verbose_name="Leave Category",
USER     )
USER     requested_from = models.DateField("Requested From")
USER     requested_to = models.DateField("Requested To")
USER     purpose = models.TextField("Purpose / Reason")
USER     station_leave_details = models.JSONField(
USER         null=True, blank=True, verbose_name="Station Leave Details"
USER     )
USER     nominated_substitute_id = models.IntegerField(
USER         null=True, blank=True, verbose_name="Nominated Substitute ID",
USER     )
USER     status = models.CharField(
USER         max_length=20,
USER         choices=[
USER             ("draft", "Draft"),
USER             ("submitted", "Submitted"),
USER             ("under_review", "Under Review"),
USER             ("recommended", "Recommended"),
USER             ("not_recommended", "Not Recommended"),
USER             ("approved", "Approved"),
USER             ("rejected", "Rejected"),
USER             ("cancelled", "Cancelled"),
USER             ("withdrawn", "Withdrawn"),
USER         ],
USER         default="draft",
USER     )
USER 
USER     class Meta:
USER         verbose_name = "Leave Application"
USER         verbose_name_plural = "Leave Applications"
USER         ordering = ["-requested_from"]
USER 
USER     def __str__(self) -> str:
USER         return f"{self.user_id}'s {self.category} application ({self.requested_from} to {self.requested_to})"
USER 
USER 
USER class LeaveBalance(UserScopedModel):
USER     """Per-user, per-year, per-category leave balance."""
USER 
USER     category = models.CharField(
USER         max_length=2,
USER         choices=LeaveCategory.choices,
USER         verbose_name="Leave Category",
USER     )
USER     year = models.IntegerField("Year")
USER     available_days = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=0.0)
USER     used_days = models.DecimalField(max_digits=5, decimal_places=2, default=0.0)
USER

USER         verbose_name = "Leave Balance"
USER         verbose_name_plural = "Leave Balances"
USER         ordering = ["-year", "-category"]
USER         unique_together = ["user_id", "year", "category"]
