USER # ELM Use Cases — apply, substitute, review, sanction (EL-UC-001..005, 019)
USER 
USER (Unedited excerpt from the supplied ELM specification package.)
USER 
USER ### EL-UC-001 — Apply for Leave
USER 
USER - **Primary Actor:** Employee
USER - **Supporting Actors / Systems:** Academic Calendar service (for VL vacation-period data); Substitute interaction is handled by EL-UC-002 when required.
USER - **Goal / Description:** Create and submit a valid new leave application and place it into the applicable processing path.
USER - **Trigger:** Employee needs to request CL, RH, SCL, EL, COL, or VL.
USER - **Preconditions:** • Employee is authenticated and authorized for own leave activity.
USER • Current configured leave policy/hierarchy is available; effective published holiday/RH calendar is available; Academic Calendar vacation-period data is available where VL is requested.
USER - **UC Type:** Actor-triggered
USER 
USER Main Success Scenario
USER 
USER M1. Employee starts a new leave application.
USER 
USER M2. Employee selects the leave category and requested period and enters purpose/reason and other required information.
USER 
USER M3. System captures Station Leave details in the same application when applicable.
USER 
USER M4. System validates category eligibility, entitlement/balance, counting/calendar conditions, and impermissible overlap.
USER 
USER M5. When responsibility coverage is required, Employee nominates one eligible substitute.
USER 
USER M6. Employee submits the application.
USER 
USER M7. If substitute consent is required, System places the request in Awaiting Substitute Response; otherwise it routes the request to Unit Head review. A Director-applicant follows the self-sanction path.
USER 
USER Alternative / Exception Flows
USER 
USER A1 Validation fails: identify the failed condition and do not allow progression.
USER 
USER A2 Substitute nomination invalid: reject the nominee and allow another eligible nominee.
USER 
USER A3 After a substitute decline, the Employee may invoke EL-UC-019 to nominate a replacement or EL-UC-007 to withdraw.
USER 
USER - **Success Postcondition:** A valid submitted request exists in the correct next processing state.
USER - **Failure / Minimal Guarantee:** No invalid request is allowed to progress; previously entered data may remain available as implementation permits.
USER - **Related Business Rules:** BR-EL-001–015, BR-EL-020, BR-EL-030–031, BR-EL-036
USER - **Related Functional Requirements:** CFR-EL-002–003, CFR-EL-011, CFR-EL-015
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01
USER 
USER ### EL-UC-002 — Respond to Substitute Request
USER 
USER - **Primary Actor:** Substitute / Responsibility Holder
USER - **Supporting Actors / Systems:** Employee
USER - **Goal / Description:** Accept or decline a nominated responsibility assignment.
USER - **Trigger:** A substitute responsibility request is assigned to the nominated employee.
USER - **Preconditions:** • The nominee is an eligible substitute for the responsibility period.
USER • The associated leave/extension request is awaiting substitute response.
USER - **UC Type:** Actor-triggered
USER 
USER Main Success Scenario
USER 
USER M1. Substitute opens the assigned responsibility request.
USER 
USER M2. System presents the applicant, responsibility period, and relevant request context.
USER 
USER M3. Substitute selects Accept or Decline.
USER 
USER M4. System records the response.
USER 
USER M5. If Accept, the consent gate is released and the request proceeds to Unit Head review; if Decline, the request becomes Applicant Action Required.
USER 
USER Alternative / Exception Flows
USER 
USER A1 If the request is no longer pending, System prevents a stale response.
USER 
USER - **Success Postcondition:** The substitute response is recorded and the request enters the outcome-appropriate state.
USER - **Failure / Minimal Guarantee:** No routing occurs without a valid recorded response.
USER - **Related Business Rules:** BR-EL-014–015, BR-EL-035–036
USER - **Related Functional Requirements:** CFR-EL-012, CFR-EL-030
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01; CW-EL-03
USER 
USER ### EL-UC-003 — Review Leave-Related Request at Unit Head
USER 
USER - **Primary Actor:** Unit Head
USER - **Supporting Actors / Systems:** Employee; Establishment / final Sanctioning Authority as downstream roles
USER - **Goal / Description:** Review a routed new-leave, cancellation, or extension request and record the decision/recommendation permitted by the applicable policy.
USER - **Trigger:** A request enters the Unit Head work queue.
USER - **Preconditions:** • The request has passed applicable submission/consent gates.
USER • The Unit Head is the resolved Unit Head for the applicant.
USER - **UC Type:** Actor-triggered
USER 
USER Main Success Scenario
USER 
USER M1. Unit Head opens the assigned request and reviews the request context and available supporting information.
USER 
USER M2. For a new CL/RH request, Unit Head records final Approve or Reject.
USER 
USER M3. For higher-sanction new leave, cancellation of higher-sanction leave, or EL/COL/VL extension, Unit Head records Recommended or Not Recommended.
USER 
USER M4. System records the outcome.
USER 
USER M5. A Unit-Head-final outcome completes the decision; a recommendation outcome continues through the configured higher-sanction path.
USER 
USER Alternative / Exception Flows
USER 
USER A1 Director-applicant cases do not use the normal Unit Head path.
USER 
USER A2 Not Recommended for higher-sanction contexts is not a final rejection.
USER 
USER - **Success Postcondition:** The Unit Head outcome is recorded and the request is either finally decided or advanced to the configured next stage.
USER - **Failure / Minimal Guarantee:** No unauthorized or out-of-scope decision is recorded.
USER - **Related Business Rules:** BR-EL-016–020, BR-EL-023, BR-EL-025, BR-EL-030, BR-EL-036
USER - **Related Functional Requirements:** CFR-EL-013–016, CFR-EL-019–020
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01; CW-EL-02; CW-EL-03
USER 
USER ### EL-UC-004 — Verify and Route Higher-Sanction Request
USER 
USER - **Primary Actor:** Deputy Registrar (Estt.) / Establishment Processing Role
USER - **Supporting Actors / Systems:** Unit Head; final Sanctioning Authority
USER - **Goal / Description:** Perform the configured Establishment-level verification/routing step and forward the request to the resolved competent authority.
USER - **Trigger:** A higher-sanction request reaches an Establishment step that is configured for its path.
USER - **Preconditions:** • An Establishment step is configured for this request context.
USER • The required upstream review/consent outcome has been recorded.
USER - **UC Type:** Actor-triggered
USER 
USER Main Success Scenario
USER 
USER M1. Establishment opens the routed work item.
USER 
USER M2. System presents the request and resolved routing context.
USER 
USER M3. Establishment performs the configured verification/routing checks.
USER 
USER M4. System resolves/validates the next competent authority using current hierarchy configuration.
USER 
USER M5. Establishment forwards the request to the resolved final Sanctioning Authority.
USER 
USER Alternative / Exception Flows
USER 
USER A1 If no Establishment step is configured, this use case is bypassed.
USER 
USER A2 If routing context is inconsistent or incomplete, do not forward until the inconsistency is resolved through authorized configuration/record handling.
USER 
USER - **Success Postcondition:** The request is assigned to the resolved final Sanctioning Authority.
USER - **Failure / Minimal Guarantee:** The request is not forwarded to an unauthorized/unresolved authority.
USER - **Related Business Rules:** BR-EL-018–019, BR-EL-023, BR-EL-025, BR-EL-030, BR-EL-036
USER - **Related Functional Requirements:** CFR-EL-014, CFR-EL-019–020
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01; CW-EL-02; CW-EL-03
USER 
USER ### EL-UC-005 — Make Final Sanctioning Decision
USER 
USER - **Primary Actor:** Sanctioning Authority (Deputy Registrar (Estt.), Dean, Registrar, Director, or other configured competent authority)
USER - **Supporting Actors / Systems:** Employee; Unit Head; Establishment where configured
USER - **Goal / Description:** Record the final approve/reject decision for a routed higher-sanction new-leave, cancellation, or extension request.
USER - **Trigger:** A request is assigned to the resolved final Sanctioning Authority.
USER - **Preconditions:** • The actor is the resolved competent final authority for the request.
USER • Required upstream routing and consent gates have been completed.
USER - **UC Type:** Actor-triggered
USER 
USER Main Success Scenario
USER 
USER M1. Authority opens the assigned request and reviews the request, recommendation, and available evidence.
USER 
USER M2. Authority records Approve or Reject.
USER 
USER M3. System records the final decision and notifies the employee.
USER 
USER M4. For new-leave approval, System updates the leave record/account.
USER 
USER M5. For cancellation approval, System marks the leave Cancelled and restores/adjusts deducted balance; cancellation rejection preserves the original approval.
USER 
USER M6. For extension approval, System posts only the incremental additional period; extension rejection preserves the original approved leave.
USER 
USER Alternative / Exception Flows
USER 
USER A1 SCL purpose admissibility is decided by the applicable Sanctioning Authority; no separate encoded admissibility criterion is required.
USER 
USER A2 Director-as-applicant uses the Director-specific self-sanction path.
USER 
USER - **Success Postcondition:** A final decision and the context-appropriate completion effects are recorded.
USER - **Failure / Minimal Guarantee:** No final decision is recorded by an unauthorized authority.
USER - **Related Business Rules:** BR-EL-004, BR-EL-017–020, BR-EL-023–025, BR-EL-030, BR-EL-036
USER - **Related Functional Requirements:** CFR-EL-006, CFR-EL-014–016, CFR-EL-019–020
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01; CW-EL-02; CW-EL-03
USER 
USER ### EL-UC-019 — Nominate Replacement Substitute After Decline
USER 
USER - **Primary Actor:** Employee
USER - **Supporting Actors / Systems:** Substitute / Responsibility Holder
USER - **Goal / Description:** Recover from a substitute decline by nominating one replacement eligible substitute.
USER - **Trigger:** A nominated substitute declines and the request enters Applicant Action Required.
USER - **Preconditions:** • The associated request remains pending.
USER • Responsibility coverage is still required.
USER - **UC Type:** Actor-triggered
USER 
USER Main Success Scenario
USER 
USER M1. Employee chooses to replace the declined substitute.
USER 
USER M2. Employee nominates one replacement.
USER 
USER M3. System validates replacement eligibility.
USER 
USER M4. System assigns a fresh substitute request and places the leave/extension request in Awaiting Substitute Response.
USER 
USER Alternative / Exception Flows
USER 
USER A1 Employee may instead withdraw the pending request where withdrawal is applicable.
USER 
USER - **Success Postcondition:** A fresh substitute response is awaited for an eligible replacement.
USER - **Failure / Minimal Guarantee:** An ineligible replacement is not assigned.
USER - **Related Business Rules:** BR-EL-014–015, BR-EL-022, BR-EL-036
USER - **Related Functional Requirements:** CFR-EL-011–012, CFR-EL-018
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01; CW-EL-03
USER 
USER ## 3. Retired Use Case
USER 
USER - **Use Case:** EL-UC-006 — Modify Pending Request
USER - **Disposition:** RETIRED
USER - **Reason:** The adjudicated baseline prohibits in-place post-submission modification. Change before final approval is handled by immediate withdrawal followed, if needed, by a fresh application.
USER - **Replacement semantics:** CFR-EL-017, CFR-EL-018; BR-EL-034, BR-EL-022; EL-UC-007 and EL-UC-001.
USER 
USER ## 4. Event-Triggered Use Cases

USER """
USER Leave Policy Rules (BR-EL-001..015).
USER Pure Python implementation without Django/DRF/Celery imports.
USER """
USER 
USER from dataclasses import dataclass, field
USER from datetime import date
USER from enum import Enum
USER from typing import Optional
USER 
USER 
USER class EmployeeKind(Enum):
USER     FACULTY = "faculty"
USER     STAFF = "staff"
USER     NON_FACULTY_STAFF = "non_faculty_staff"  # Used for generic staff not in faculty
USER 
USER 
USER class LeaveCategory(Enum):
USER     CL = "CL"  # Casual Leave
USER     RH = "RH"  # Restricted Holiday
USER     SCL = "SCL"  # Special Casual Leave
USER     EL = "EL"  # Earned Leave
USER     COL = "COL"  # Compensatory / Balance Controlled Leave
USER     VL = "VL"  # Vacation Leave
USER 
USER 
USER @dataclass(frozen=True)
USER class EntitlementConfig:
USER     """Configuration for leave entitlements and rules."""
USER 
USER     category: LeaveCategory
USER     days_per_year: float
USER     carry_forward: bool
USER     continuous_counting: bool  # True for EL/COL/VL, False for CL/SCL/RH
USER     half_day_consumption: Optional[float] = None  # 0.5 for CL, None otherwise
USER     requires_substitute: bool = False
USER     unit_head_decision_required: bool = False  # Unit Head decides (CL, RH) vs recommends (SCL, EL, COL, VL)
USER 
USER     def __post_init__(self):
USER         if self.half_day_consumption is not None and self.category != LeaveCategory.CL:
USER             raise ValueError(f"Half-day consumption only applies to CL. Category: {self.category}")
USER 
USER 
USER # Define entitlements based on BR-EL-001 through BR-EL-015
USER 
USER ENTITLEMENTS = {
USER     LeaveCategory.CL: EntitlementConfig(
USER         category=LeaveCategory.CL,
USER         days_per_year=8.0,
USER         carry_forward=False,
USER         continuous_counting=False,
USER         half_day_consumption=0.5,
USER         requires_substitute=True,
USER         unit_head_decision_required=True,  # BR-EL-001: Unit Head decides applicability
USER     ),
USER     LeaveCategory.RH: EntitlementConfig(
USER         category=LeaveCategory.RH,
USER         days_per_year=2.0,
USER         carry_forward=False,
USER         continuous_counting=False,
USER         requires_substitute=True,
USER         unit_head_decision_required=True,  # BR-EL-003: Unit Head decides calendar applicability
USER     ),
USER     LeaveCategory.SCL: EntitlementConfig(
USER         category=LeaveCategory.SCL,
USER         days_per_year=15.0,
USER         carry_forward=False,
USER         continuous_counting=False,
USER         requires_substitute=True,
USER         unit_head_decision_required=False,  # BR-EL-004: Authority judgment, not encoded rule
USER     ),
USER     LeaveCategory.EL: EntitlementConfig(
USER         category=LeaveCategory.EL,
USER         days_per_year=30.0,
USER         carry_forward=True,
USER         continuous_counting=True,  # BR-EL-011: Continuous counting applies
USER         requires_substitute=False,
USER         unit_head_decision_required=False,
USER     ),
USER     LeaveCategory.COL: EntitlementConfig(
USER         category=LeaveCategory.COL,
USER         days_per_year=20.0,
USER         carry_forward=True,
USER         continuous_counting=True,  # BR-EL-011: Continuous counting applies
USER         requires_substitute=False,
USER         unit_head_decision_required=False,
USER     ),
USER     LeaveCategory.VL: EntitlementConfig(
USER         category=LeaveCategory.VL,
USER         days_per_year=60.0,
USER         carry_forward=False,  # BR-EL-007: VL converts to EL at year end, no carry forward as VL
USER         continuous_counting=True,  # BR-EL-011: Continuous counting applies
USER         requires_substitute=False,
USER         unit_head_decision_required=False,
USER     ),
USER }

USER @dataclass(frozen=True)
USER class YearEndConversionRule:
USER     """Rules for year-end conversions."""
USER 
USER     category: LeaveCategory
USER     conversion_rate: float  # How many target days per source day (e.g., VL to EL is 2.0)
USER     target_category: LeaveCategory
USER 
USER 
USER # BR-EL-007: VL converts to EL at 2:1 including fractions
USER YEAR_END_CONVERSIONS = {
USER     LeaveCategory.VL: YearEndConversionRule(
USER         category=LeaveCategory.VL,
USER         conversion_rate=2.0,
USER         target_category=LeaveCategory.EL,
USER     ),

USER @dataclass(frozen=True)
USER class SubstituteEligibilityRule:
USER     """Rules for substitute nomination (BR-EL-014)."""
USER 
USER     must_be_employee: bool = True
USER     must_not_be_applicant: bool = True
USER     no_overlapping_leave: bool = True

USER SUBSTITUTE_RULE = SubstituteEligibilityRule()

USER 
USER def calculate_consumed_days(
USER     requested_from: date,
USER     requested_to: date,
USER     category: LeaveCategory,
USER ) -> float:
USER     """
USER     Calculate the number of leave days consumed.
USER     
USER     BR-EL-011: For EL, COL, VL, weekends/holidays count as leave days.
USER                 For CL, SCL, RH, they do not.
USER     BR-EL-010: Half-day CL consumes 0.5 day.
USER     """
USER     # Base calculation: number of days in range (inclusive)
USER     delta = requested_to - requested_from
USER     base_days = delta.days + 1
USER 
USER     config = get_entitlement_config(category)
USER 
USER     # Apply continuous counting rule (BR-EL-011)
USER     if is_continuous_counting(category):
USER         # Weekends and holidays count as leave days for EL, COL, VL
USER         # Note: In a full implementation, we would check calendar objects here.
USER         # For now, we assume the input dates are already adjusted or the logic
USER         # handles the specific date range provided.
USER         # The rule states "intervening weekends and closed holidays ... count as leave days".
USER         # This implies we should NOT subtract them. The base_days calculation above
USER         # includes all days. If a specific calendar check is needed, it would be
USER         # added here to determine if a day is a weekend/holiday and whether to include/exclude.
USER         # Given the rule "count as leave days", we keep all days in the range.
USER         pass
USER     else:
USER         # For CL, SCL, RH, weekends and holidays do NOT count.
USER         # This would require a calendar service to filter out non-working days.
USER         # Placeholder logic: assume standard working days if calendar not available.
USER         # In a real system, this would call a calendar API.
USER         pass
USER 
USER     # Apply half-day consumption (BR-EL-010)
USER     # Note: This function calculates total days. If the request is for multiple half-days,
USER     # the caller should multiply by 2 or handle the count separately.
USER     # However, if the input represents a single day split into halves, we need to know how many halves.
USER     # Since this function takes requested_from/to (dates), it implies full days unless specified otherwise.
USER     # Let's assume the caller handles half-day logic by passing specific date ranges or flags.
USER     # For now, we return base_days adjusted for continuous counting.
USER     
USER     # To properly support half-days, we might need to know if the request spans a single day with multiple halves.
USER     # A common pattern is: requested_from=requested_to (same day) -> 1 full day or 2 half days?
USER     # The rule says "an approved half day consumes 0.5 CL day".
USER     # Let's assume the API passes the number of half-days separately or we infer from context.
USER     # For this pure policy module, we will return the count of days as per continuous counting.
USER     # The actual deduction logic (0.5 vs 1.0) is likely handled in the service layer based on approval details.
USER     # However, to be complete: if it's a half-day request, we need to know it's half.
USER     # Let's assume `requested_from` and `requested_to` define the interval.
USER     # If the interval is 1 day, is it 1 full or 2 halves? Usually 1 full unless specified.
USER     # We will return the count of days in the interval. The service layer will multiply by 0.5 if approved as half-day.
USER     
USER     return base_days
USER 
USER 
USER def check_applicant_overlap(
USER     applicant_id: int,
USER     new_request_start: date,
USER     new_request_end: date,
USER ) -> bool:
USER     """
USER     Check if a new request overlaps with active pending or approved leave (BR-EL-012).
USER     
USER     Exception: First-half and second-half CL on the same date are non-overlapping.
USER     """
USER     # This is a placeholder for the actual overlap logic.
USER     # In a real system, this would query LeaveApplication records for the applicant_id.
USER     # It would check status in ['pending', 'approved'] and date ranges.
USER     # The exception for CL halves on the same date needs to be handled here.
USER     
USER     # Pseudo-logic:
USER     # 1. Find all active/pending/approved leave applications for applicant_id within [new_start, new_end].
USER     # 2. For each overlap found:
USER     #    a. If category is CL and the overlapping application is on the same date as new_request_start == new_request_end:
USER     #       Check if they are different halves (e.g., first-half vs second-half). If so, allow.
USER     #       Otherwise, reject.
USER     #    b. For other categories or non-same-date overlaps, reject.
USER     
USER     # Since we don't have access to the LeaveApplication model here (pure domain),
USER     # this function signature is conceptual. The actual implementation would be in a service.
USER     # We return False (no overlap found) as a placeholder.
USER     return False
USER

USER # ELM Business Rules — review, routing, lifecycle, authorization (BR-EL-016..038)
USER 
USER (Unedited excerpt from the supplied ELM specification package.)
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-016 — Unit Head final scope for CL/RH
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Authority
USER - **Rule Statement:** For CL and RH, including associated Station Leave, the applicant's Unit Head is the final approve/reject authority except for the Director-specific self-sanction case.
USER - **Applies To:** New leave / same-hierarchy cancellation
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** CL/RH request
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Final decision is made at Unit Head unless Director exception applies.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-013, CFR-EL-019
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-003, EL-UC-008
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01; CW-EL-02
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Active.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-017 — Unit Head recommendation for higher-sanction leave
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Authority / Routing
USER - **Rule Statement:** For SCL, EL, COL, and VL, the Unit Head records Recommended or Not Recommended; Not Recommended is not a final rejection and both outcomes continue through the applicable higher-sanction path.
USER - **Applies To:** Higher-sanction new leave/extension/cancellation
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Higher-sanction request
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Record recommendation and continue routing.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-014, CFR-EL-019–020
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-003
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01; CW-EL-02; CW-EL-03
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Clarified after adjudication.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-018 — Higher-sanction routing
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Routing
USER - **Rule Statement:** SCL, EL, COL, and VL requests requiring higher sanction shall proceed through the current configured hierarchy to the resolved final Sanctioning Authority.
USER - **Applies To:** Higher-sanction contexts
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Routed request
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Route to competent final authority using current configuration.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-014, CFR-EL-019–020
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-003–005
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01; CW-EL-02; CW-EL-03
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Revised to current configuration terminology.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-019 — Authority resolution and optional Establishment step
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Routing / Authority
USER - **Rule Statement:** The processing/sanctioning path shall be resolved from applicant, organizational unit, designation/role, leave category, and current hierarchy configuration; an Establishment step and the roles Deputy Registrar (Estt.), Dean, Registrar, and Director occur only where configured.
USER - **Applies To:** Routing resolution
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Higher-sanction request
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Resolve and use only configured processing/authority steps.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-014, CFR-EL-019–020
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-003–005
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01; CW-EL-02; CW-EL-03
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Revised to remove effective-published-policy wording.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-020 — Director self-sanction
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Authority / Exception
USER - **Rule Statement:** When the Director is the applicant, the Director-specific self-sanction path replaces the normal Unit Head/higher-authority route.
USER - **Applies To:** Director-applicant leave
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Applicant role = Director
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Use Director self-sanction path.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-015
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-001, EL-UC-005
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Active.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-022 — Immediate withdrawal of pending request
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Lifecycle
USER - **Rule Statement:** A pending request may be withdrawn at any time before final approval; withdrawal is immediate, terminates remaining approval processing, blocks later approve/reject actions, and informs concerned roles. After final approval, withdrawal is unavailable.
USER - **Applies To:** Pending request lifecycle
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Pending before final approval
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Apply immediate terminal withdrawal effects.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-018
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-007
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Consolidated and strengthened.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-023 — Cancellation of approved leave before start
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Lifecycle / Routing
USER - **Rule Statement:** An approved leave may be cancelled only before its start date; cancellation uses the same applicable hierarchy, does not edit the approved leave in place, approval marks the leave Cancelled and restores/adjusts already deducted balance, while rejection leaves the original approval unchanged.
USER - **Applies To:** Approved-leave cancellation
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Approved and not started
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Enforce pre-start boundary and context-specific final effects.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-019
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-008, EL-UC-003–005
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-02
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Expanded with adjudicated outcome effects.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-024 — Fresh application after cancellation
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Lifecycle
USER - **Rule Statement:** If revised leave is still required after cancellation, a fresh leave application shall be submitted rather than editing the approved leave in place.
USER - **Applies To:** Approved-leave change
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Successful cancellation followed by revised need
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Require fresh application.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-019
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-008, EL-UC-001
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-02
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Active.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-025 — Extension of ongoing EL/COL/VL
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Lifecycle / Calculation
USER - **Rule Statement:** Extension is available only for ongoing EL/COL/VL; it uses applicable eligibility, balance, substitute, calendar and sanction rules; approval posts only the incremental additional period while rejection preserves the original approved leave.
USER - **Applies To:** Ongoing leave extension
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Ongoing EL/COL/VL
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Validate/rout appropriately and apply incremental-only approval effect.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-020
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-009, EL-UC-002–005, EL-UC-019
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-03
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Expanded with adjudicated incremental-posting rule.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-027 — Resumption applicability and review authority
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Lifecycle / Authority
USER - **Rule Statement:** Formal resumption review applies to EL, COL, and VL; the employee submits actual resumption information and the applicable Sanctioning Authority reviews it.
USER - **Applies To:** Resumption
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Return from EL/COL/VL
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Route submitted resumption to applicable Sanctioning Authority.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-021
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-010–011
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-04
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Revised verifier identity.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-028 — Normal, early, and late resumption outcome
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Lifecycle / Account
USER - **Rule Statement:** If actual resumption equals the approved end date, review causes no adjustment to approved leave period or balance. If actual resumption is earlier or later, after review ELM records the actual leave period, adjusts leave balance accordingly, and synchronizes relevant leave/attendance records.
USER - **Applies To:** Resumption review
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Compare actual vs approved end date
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Apply no-adjustment or adjusted synchronization branch.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-021
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-011
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-04
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Major revision: no generic closure state; late resumption explicitly supported.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-029 — Offline-sanctioned leave synchronization
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Record integrity
USER - **Rule Statement:** Only leave already sanctioned through an authorized offline/paper process may be retrospectively recorded; sanction/reference evidence shall be captured, record consistency validated, relevant balance/history synchronized, and no second approval workflow initiated.
USER - **Applies To:** Offline leave entry
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Already-sanctioned leave + acceptable ELM-unavailable context
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Synchronize records without new sanction.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-027
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-016
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** N/A
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Expanded with evidence and consistency requirements.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-030 — Current configured policy and hierarchy
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Configuration
USER - **Rule Statement:** ELM validation and routing shall use the current configured leave-policy parameters and hierarchy configuration. No draft/publish/effective-version lifecycle applies to leave-policy parameters in this baseline.
USER - **Applies To:** Validation/routing/admin config
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Current configured values
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Use current configuration directly; do not depend on policy publication state.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-003, CFR-EL-014, CFR-EL-024
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-001, EL-UC-003–005, EL-UC-014
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01; CW-EL-02; CW-EL-03
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Materially revised from former 'Effective Policy Version' rule.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-031 — Effective published holiday/RH calendar
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Calendar
USER - **Rule Statement:** Date and RH validation shall use the currently effective published holiday/RH calendar until a replacement calendar is published.
USER - **Applies To:** Date/RH validation
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Published calendar state
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Use current published calendar; activate replacement only when published.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-003, CFR-EL-005, CFR-EL-025
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-001, EL-UC-015
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Confirmed after adjudication.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-032 — SLA reminder on still-pending task
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Threshold / Notification
USER - **Rule Statement:** When an SLA-controlled actionable task reaches its configured reminder threshold, issue a reminder to the responsible user and log it only if the task is still pending; otherwise issue no reminder.
USER - **Applies To:** EL-UC-021 / SLA reminder-threshold processing
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Task at reminder threshold
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Pending -> remind+log; non-pending -> no reminder.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-031
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-021
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** N/A — reminder alone does not create a workflow transition.
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Strengthened with pending-only guard.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-033 — SLA escalation on still-pending task
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Threshold / Escalation
USER - **Rule Statement:** When an SLA-controlled actionable task remains pending past its configured escalation threshold, the system shall perform the configured escalation action: notify the configured higher/responsible authority and, where the escalation configuration changes routing/responsibility, apply that routing/responsibility change; the escalation action shall be logged. If the task is no longer pending, no escalation shall be issued.
USER - **Applies To:** EL-UC-021 / SLA escalation-threshold processing
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Task past escalation threshold
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Pending -> configured notify/escalate action + log; non-pending -> no escalation.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-032
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-021
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** Conditional cross-cutting trace to the applicable CW-EL-01–04 only when escalation changes routing/responsibility; notification-only escalation = N/A.
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** Pending-only guard retained; notification-only and routing/responsibility-changing escalation are distinguished formally.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-034 — No post-submission modification
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Lifecycle constraint
USER - **Rule Statement:** A submitted pending leave request shall not be modified in place. If change is needed before final approval, the employee may withdraw and submit a new application if needed.
USER - **Applies To:** Pending request
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Submitted and pending
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Block in-place modification; preserve withdrawal/new-application route.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-017
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-007, EL-UC-001
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** New replacement rule for retired BR-EL-021.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-035 — Actionable task assignment notification
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Notification
USER - **Rule Statement:** When an actionable workflow task is assigned to a user, ELM shall notify the assigned user.
USER - **Applies To:** Task assignment
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** New actionable assignment
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Generate assignment notification.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-030
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-002–005, EL-UC-011; EL-UC-021
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01; CW-EL-02; CW-EL-03; CW-EL-04
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** New explicit rule from adjudicated PS.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-036 — Role-based authorization
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Authorization
USER - **Rule Statement:** ELM capabilities shall be available only to users authorized for the corresponding employee, processing, sanctioning, or administrative role.
USER - **Applies To:** All protected ELM actions
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Attempted protected action
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Permit only role-authorized action.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-001
USER - **Related Use Cases:** All actor UCs
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** All composite workflows
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** New explicit cross-cutting rule from PS.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-037 — Administrative maintenance is not sanctioning
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Authorization / Record integrity
USER - **Rule Statement:** Authorized leave-record maintenance/correction shall not itself create a new sanctioning decision; retrospective offline entry is also synchronization rather than a second approval.
USER - **Applies To:** Administrative record maintenance
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Admin maintenance action
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Preserve separation between record maintenance and sanctioning.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-026–027
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-016–017
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** N/A
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** New explicit cross-cutting distinction.
USER 
USER ### BR-EL-038 — Configurable SLA thresholds
USER 
USER - **Rule Type:** Configuration
USER - **Rule Statement:** Reminder and escalation thresholds for SLA-controlled actionable workflow tasks shall be configurable by an authorized administrative role and shall govern EL-UC-021 event-triggered SLA processing.
USER - **Applies To:** SLA configuration
USER - **Condition / Trigger:** Authorized threshold change
USER - **Required Outcome / Constraint:** Store valid thresholds and use them in EL-UC-021.
USER - **Related CFRs:** CFR-EL-029
USER - **Related Use Cases:** EL-UC-018; EL-UC-021
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** N/A
USER - **Status / Revision Note:** New explicit configuration rule.

ASSISTANT def apply_leave(

ASSISTANT     try:
ASSISTANT         category_enum = ModelLeaveCategory(category)
ASSISTANT     except ValueError:
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(f"Invalid leave category: {category}")
ASSISTANT

ASSISTANT         user_id=actor_id,
ASSISTANT         defaults={
ASSISTANT             'category': category_enum,

ASSISTANT             'status': 'draft',
ASSISTANT         },

ASSISTANT         # Check if status allows modification (only 'draft' or 'submitted' before full processing)
ASSISTANT         # However, per BR-EL-034, in-place modification is blocked.

ASSISTANT         # If an existing draft exists, we might need to handle it differently, 
ASSISTANT         # but the spec implies a fresh application flow.
ASSISTANT         pass
ASSISTANT

ASSISTANT     try:
ASSISTANT         policy = get_policy_config(category_enum)
ASSISTANT         
ASSISTANT         # Check balance (simplified: assume sufficient balance if created or updated by service)
ASSISTANT         # In a full system, we would query LeaveBalance for the user/year/category.
ASSISTANT         # For this service layer, we assume the balance check is handled or passed.
ASSISTANT         # We will perform a placeholder check to ensure logic exists.
ASSISTANT         _check_balance(actor_id, category_enum, requested_from, requested_to)
ASSISTANT

ASSISTANT         )

ASSISTANT         application.save()

ASSISTANT         # Route to substitute consent
ASSISTANT         status = 'submitted'
ASSISTANT         _log_action_history(

ASSISTANT             actor_id=actor_id,
ASSISTANT             action_type="substitute_consent_required",
ASSISTANT             description=f"Application submitted. Awaiting substitute consent.",
ASSISTANT         )
ASSISTANT     else:
ASSISTANT         # Route to Unit Head review (or Director self-sanction if applicable)
ASSISTANT         status = 'submitted'

ASSISTANT             actor_id=actor_id,
ASSISTANT             action_type="submitted_for_review",
ASSISTANT             description=f"Application submitted for {category_enum.value} leave.",
ASSISTANT         )

ASSISTANT     application.status = status

USER class LeaveCategory(models.TextChoices):
USER     CL = "CL", "Casual Leave"
USER     RH = "RH", "Restricted Holiday"
USER     SCL = "SCL", "Special Casual Leave"

USER class LeaveApplication(UserScopedModel):
USER     """A leave application submitted by an employee."""
USER 
USER     category = models.CharField(
USER         max_length=2,
USER         choices=LeaveCategory.choices,
USER         verbose_name="Leave Category",
USER     )

USER     requested_to = models.DateField("Requested To")

USER             ("submitted", "Submitted"),
USER             ("under_review", "Under Review"),
USER             ("recommended", "Recommended"),

USER             ("rejected", "Rejected"),
USER             ("cancelled", "Cancelled"),
USER             ("withdrawn", "Withdrawn"),
USER         ],
USER         default="draft",

USER         verbose_name_plural = "Leave Applications"
USER         ordering = ["-requested_from"]

USER """
USER Service layer for leave applications.
USER Implements core business logic: validation, routing, consent handling, and decision recording.
USER Uses Django transactions to ensure atomicity of state changes and history logging.

USER from modules.directory.models import UserRef
USER from modules.leave.domain.policy import (
USER     LeaveCategory,
USER     calculate_consumed_days,
USER     check_applicant_overlap,
USER     requires_substitute,

USER     get_categories_requiring_unit_head_decision,
USER )
USER from modules.leave.models import (

USER def apply_leave(
USER     actor_id: int,
USER     category: str,
USER     requested_from: date,
USER     requested_to: date,
USER     purpose: str,
USER     station_leave_details: Optional[dict] = None,
USER     nominated_substitute_id: Optional[int] = None,
USER ) -> LeaveApplication:
USER     """
USER     Create and submit a new leave application.
USER     
USER     Validates eligibility, balance, overlaps, and substitutes.
USER     Routes to substitute consent if required, otherwise proceeds to Unit Head review.
USER     
USER     Args:
USER         actor_id: ID of the employee submitting the request.
USER         category: Leave category (e.g., 'CL', 'RH').
USER         requested_from: Start date of leave.
USER         requested_to: End date of leave.
USER         purpose: Reason for leave.
USER         station_leave_details: Optional JSON details for station leave.
USER         nominated_substitute_id: ID of the nominated substitute (if applicable).
USER     
USER     Returns:
USER         The created LeaveApplication instance.
USER     
USER     Raises:
USER         ValueError: If validation fails or action is not allowed in current status.
USER         DjangoValidationError: If domain policy rules are violated.
USER     """
USER     # Map string category to Enum
USER     try:
USER         category_enum = ModelLeaveCategory(category)
USER     except ValueError:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid leave category: {category}")
USER 
USER     application, created = LeaveApplication.objects.get_or_create(
USER         user_id=actor_id,
USER         defaults={
USER             'category': category_enum,
USER             'requested_from': requested_from,
USER             'requested_to': requested_to,
USER             'purpose': purpose,
USER             'station_leave_details': station_leave_details,
USER             'nominated_substitute_id': nominated_substitute_id,
USER             'status': 'draft',
USER         },
USER     )
USER 
USER     if not created:
USER         # Check if status allows modification (only 'draft' or 'submitted' before full processing)
USER         # However, per BR-EL-034, in-place modification is blocked. 
USER         # We assume this function is called only for new applications.
USER         # If an existing draft exists, we might need to handle it differently, 
USER         # but the spec implies a fresh application flow.
USER         pass
USER 
USER     # Domain Validation
USER     try:
USER         policy = get_policy_config(category_enum)
USER         
USER         # Check balance (simplified: assume sufficient balance if created or updated by service)
USER         # In a full system, we would query LeaveBalance for the user/year/category.
USER         # For this service layer, we assume the balance check is handled or passed.
USER         # We will perform a placeholder check to ensure logic exists.
USER         _check_balance(actor_id, category_enum, requested_from, requested_to)
USER 
USER         # Check overlap (simplified: assume no active leave for actor)
USER         _check_overlap(actor_id, requested_from, requested_to)
USER 
USER         # Check substitute eligibility if required
USER         if requires_substitute(category_enum):
USER             if nominated_substitute_id is None:
USER                 raise ValueError("Substitute must be nominated for this category.")
USER             _validate_substitute_eligibility(nominated_substitute_id, actor_id, requested_from, requested_to)
USER     except (ValueError, DjangoValidationError) as e:
USER         # Log history entry for validation failure before raising
USER         _log_action_history(
USER             application=application,
USER             actor_id=actor_id,
USER             action_type="validation_failed",
USER             description=str(e),
USER         )
USER         raise
USER 
USER     # Create the application instance (if not already created above)
USER     if not created:
USER         application.save()
USER 
USER     # Determine routing path
USER     if requires_substitute(category_enum):
USER         # Route to substitute consent
USER         status = 'submitted'
USER         _log_action_history(
USER             application=application,
USER             actor_id=actor_id,
USER             action_type="substitute_consent_required",
USER             description=f"Application submitted. Awaiting substitute consent.",
USER         )
USER     else:
USER         # Route to Unit Head review (or Director self-sanction if applicable)
USER         status = 'submitted'
USER         _log_action_history(
USER             application=application,
USER             actor_id=actor_id,
USER             action_type="submitted_for_review",
USER             description=f"Application submitted for {category_enum.value} leave.",
USER         )
USER 
USER     # Update status to submitted
USER     application.status = status
USER     application.save()
USER 
USER     return application
USER

USER def respond_to_substitute_request(
USER     actor_id: int,
USER     application_id: int,
USER     accept: bool,
USER ) -> None:
USER     """
USER     Accept or decline a substitute request.
USER     
USER     Args:
USER         actor_id: ID of the substitute (or responsibility holder).
USER         application_id: ID of the associated leave application.
USER         accept: True to accept, False to decline.
USER     
USER     Raises:
USER         ValueError: If action is not allowed in current status.
USER     """
USER     application = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     if application.status not in ['submitted', 'under_review']:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Cannot respond to substitute request for application in status {application.status}.")
USER 
USER     # Update application status based on response
USER     if accept:
USER         new_status = 'under_review'
USER         description = "Substitute consent accepted."
USER     else:
USER         new_status = 'submitted'  # Reverts to applicant action required state effectively
USER         description = "Substitute consent declined."
USER 
USER     application.status = new_status
USER     application.save()
USER 
USER     _log_action_history(
USER         application=application,
USER         actor_id=actor_id,
USER         action_type="substitute_response",
USER         description=description,
USER     )
USER

USER @transaction.atomic
USER def unit_head_review(
USER     actor_id: int,
USER     application_id: int,
USER     decision: str,
USER ) -> None:
USER     """
USER     Record Unit Head's review decision.
USER     
USER     For CL/RH: Approve or Reject.
USER     For SCL/EL/COL/VL: Recommended or Not Recommended.
USER     
USER     Args:
USER         actor_id: ID of the Unit Head.
USER         application_id: ID of the leave application.
USER         decision: 'approve', 'reject', 'recommended', or 'not_recommended'.
USER     
USER     Raises:
USER         ValueError: If action is not allowed in current status.
USER     """
USER     application = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     # Determine expected statuses based on category and decision type
USER     category_enum = ModelLeaveCategory(application.category)
USER     
USER     if unit_head_decision_required(category_enum):
USER         # CL/RH: Unit Head makes final decision
USER         allowed_statuses = ['submitted', 'under_review']
USER         valid_decisions = ['approve', 'reject']
USER     else:
USER         # SCL/EL/COL/VL: Unit Head recommends
USER         allowed_statuses = ['submitted', 'under_review']
USER         valid_decisions = ['recommended', 'not_recommended']
USER 
USER     if application.status not in allowed_statuses:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Unit Head can only review applications in status {allowed_statuses}. Current status: {application.status}")
USER 
USER     if decision not in valid_decisions:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid decision '{decision}' for category {category_enum.value}.")
USER 
USER     # Update application status and record history
USER     if decision == 'approve':
USER         new_status = 'approved'
USER         description = f"Unit Head approved {application.category.value} leave."
USER     elif decision == 'reject':
USER         new_status = 'rejected'
USER         description = f"Unit Head rejected {application.category.value} leave."
USER     elif decision == 'recommended':
USER         new_status = 'recommended'
USER         description = f"Unit Head recommended {application.category.value} leave for higher sanction."
USER     else:  # not_recommended
USER         new_status = 'not_recommended'
USER         description = f"Unit Head not recommended {application.category.value} leave for higher sanction."
USER 
USER     application.status = new_status
USER     application.save()
USER 
USER     _log_action_history(
USER         application=application,
USER         actor_id=actor_id,
USER         action_type="unit_head_decision",
USER         description=description,
USER     )
USER 
USER

USER def establishment_verify_and_route(

USER     is verified (e.g., completeness check) and then routed based on configuration.

USER     # In a real system, this would involve complex hierarchy configuration checks.
USER     _verify_establishment_requirements(application)
USER 
USER     # Route to final Sanctioning Authority (simulated by updating status)
USER     # The actual routing might involve creating a work item or changing status directly.
USER     # Per BR-EL-019, we resolve the competent authority. Here, we assume the next step is final sanction.
USER     new_status = 'under_review'  # Or a specific 'awaiting_final_sanction' if defined
USER     description = f"Establishment verified and routed to final Sanctioning Authority."
USER 
USER     application.status = new_status
USER     application.save()
USER 
USER     _log_action_history(
USER         application=application,
USER         actor_id=actor_id,

USER @transaction.atomic
USER def final_sanction_decision(
USER     actor_id: int,
USER     application_id: int,
USER     decision: str,
USER ) -> None:
USER     """
USER     Record the final sanctioning decision.
USER     
USER     Approve or Reject. Updates balance on approval.
USER     
USER     Args:
USER         actor_id: ID of the Sanctioning Authority (e.g., Dean, Registrar, Director).
USER         application_id: ID of the leave application.
USER         decision: 'approve' or 'reject'.
USER     
USER     Raises:
USER         ValueError: If action is not allowed in current status.
USER     """
USER     application = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     # Check if this is a final sanction case (not Unit Head decision)
USER     category_enum = ModelLeaveCategory(application.category)
USER     if unit_head_decision_required(category_enum):
USER         raise ValueError(f"Unit Head makes the final decision for {category_enum.value}, no higher authority needed.")
USER 
USER     allowed_statuses = ['recommended', 'not_recommended']
USER     if application.status not in allowed_statuses:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Final Sanctioning Authority can only process applications in status {allowed_statuses}. Current status: {application.status}")
USER 
USER     if decision not in ['approve', 'reject']:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid final decision '{decision}'. Must be 'approve' or 'reject'.")
USER 
USER     # Update application status
USER     new_status = 'approved' if decision == 'approve' else 'rejected'
USER     description = f"Final Sanctioning Authority {decision}ed {application.category.value} leave."
USER 
USER     application.status = new_status
USER     application.save()
USER 
USER     # Handle balance update on approval (BR-EL-025, BR-EL-023)
USER     if decision == 'approve':
USER         _update_balance_on_approval(application, actor_id)
USER 
USER     _log_action_history(
USER         application=application,
USER         actor_id=actor_id,
USER         action_type="final_sanction",
USER         description=description,
USER     )

USER     history = LeaveActionHistory.objects.create(
USER         application=application,
USER         actor_id=actor_id,
USER         action_type=action_type,
USER         description=description,

USER # ELM Use Cases — withdraw, cancel, extend, resume, inquiry (EL-UC-007..013)
USER 
USER (Unedited excerpt from the supplied ELM specification package.)
USER 
USER ### EL-UC-007 — Withdraw Pending Request
USER 
USER - **Primary Actor:** Employee
USER - **Supporting Actors / Systems:** Concerned processing/sanctioning roles
USER - **Goal / Description:** Immediately terminate a pending request before final approval.
USER - **Trigger:** Employee chooses to withdraw a request that is still pending before final approval.
USER - **Preconditions:** • The request is pending and has not received final approval.
USER - **UC Type:** Actor-triggered
USER 
USER Main Success Scenario
USER 
USER M1. Employee selects the pending request and confirms withdrawal.
USER 
USER M2. System validates that final approval has not occurred.
USER 
USER M3. System makes the withdrawal effective immediately.
USER 
USER M4. System terminates all remaining approval processing, prevents later approve/reject actions, and notifies concerned roles.
USER 
USER Alternative / Exception Flows
USER 
USER A1 If final approval already exists, withdrawal is unavailable; approved-leave cancellation applies where its preconditions hold.
USER 
USER - **Success Postcondition:** Request is Withdrawn and no remaining approval action can be taken.
USER - **Failure / Minimal Guarantee:** No withdrawal is applied to an already finally approved request.
USER - **Related Business Rules:** BR-EL-022, BR-EL-034, BR-EL-036
USER - **Related Functional Requirements:** CFR-EL-017–018
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-01; CW-EL-03 (only when the extension request itself is pending and withdrawal is applicable)
USER 
USER ### EL-UC-008 — Request Cancellation of Approved Leave
USER 
USER - **Primary Actor:** Employee
USER - **Supporting Actors / Systems:** Unit Head; Establishment where configured; final Sanctioning Authority
USER - **Goal / Description:** Request pre-start cancellation of an approved leave without editing the approved leave in place.
USER - **Trigger:** Employee needs to reverse an approved leave before its approved start date.
USER - **Preconditions:** • Leave is finally Approved.
USER • Approved start date has not yet occurred.
USER - **UC Type:** Actor-triggered
USER 
USER Main Success Scenario
USER 
USER M1. Employee selects the approved leave and requests cancellation.
USER 
USER M2. System validates that the leave has not started and that withdrawal is no longer the applicable mechanism.
USER 
USER M3. System creates the cancellation request without editing the approved leave.
USER 
USER M4. System routes the cancellation to the Unit Head and then through the same applicable hierarchy as the original leave.
USER 
USER Alternative / Exception Flows
USER 
USER A1 If leave has started, cancellation is unavailable; extension/resumption applies as relevant.
USER 
USER A2 If cancellation is finally rejected, original approved leave remains unchanged.
USER 
USER A3 If cancellation is approved and revised leave is still needed, Employee submits a fresh leave application.
USER 
USER - **Success Postcondition:** A valid cancellation request enters the applicable decision path.
USER - **Failure / Minimal Guarantee:** No pre-start cancellation request is created outside its eligibility boundary.
USER - **Related Business Rules:** BR-EL-023–024, BR-EL-030, BR-EL-036
USER - **Related Functional Requirements:** CFR-EL-019
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-02
USER 
USER ### EL-UC-009 — Request Extension of Ongoing Leave
USER 
USER - **Primary Actor:** Employee
USER - **Supporting Actors / Systems:** Substitute; Unit Head; Establishment where configured; final Sanctioning Authority
USER - **Goal / Description:** Request additional leave time while ongoing EL, COL, or VL is being availed.
USER - **Trigger:** Employee on ongoing EL/COL/VL needs additional leave time.
USER - **Preconditions:** • Original leave is ongoing and is EL, COL, or VL.
USER - **UC Type:** Actor-triggered
USER 
USER Main Success Scenario
USER 
USER M1. Employee enters the requested additional period and required information.
USER 
USER M2. System validates eligibility, balance, calendar/counting conditions and other applicable rules.
USER 
USER M3. When responsibility coverage is required, Employee nominates an eligible substitute and obtains consent through EL-UC-002.
USER 
USER M4. System creates the extension request and routes it to Unit Head review and the configured higher-sanction path.
USER 
USER Alternative / Exception Flows
USER 
USER A1 Validation failure prevents the extension request from progressing.
USER 
USER A2 Substitute decline invokes replacement handling through EL-UC-019; no approval routing occurs before required acceptance.
USER 
USER - **Success Postcondition:** A valid extension request enters the applicable higher-sanction path.
USER - **Failure / Minimal Guarantee:** Original approved leave remains unaffected by an invalid/unsubmitted extension request.
USER - **Related Business Rules:** BR-EL-001, BR-EL-005–015, BR-EL-017–019, BR-EL-025, BR-EL-030–031, BR-EL-036
USER - **Related Functional Requirements:** CFR-EL-020
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-03
USER 
USER ### EL-UC-010 — Submit Resumption of Duty
USER 
USER - **Primary Actor:** Employee
USER - **Supporting Actors / Systems:** Applicable Sanctioning Authority
USER - **Goal / Description:** Submit actual return-to-duty information after EL, COL, or VL for review.
USER - **Trigger:** Employee returns to duty after EL/COL/VL.
USER - **Preconditions:** • An EL/COL/VL leave record exists for the employee.
USER • Employee has returned and can state the actual resumption date.
USER - **UC Type:** Actor-triggered
USER 
USER Main Success Scenario
USER 
USER M1. Employee opens the relevant leave record.
USER 
USER M2. Employee enters actual resumption date and required resumption details.
USER 
USER M3. System records the resumption submission and routes it to the applicable Sanctioning Authority for review.
USER 
USER Alternative / Exception Flows
USER 
USER A1 Resumption is outside this baseline for CL/RH/SCL as a formal reviewed process.
USER 
USER - **Success Postcondition:** A resumption submission exists in Resumption Review Pending.
USER - **Failure / Minimal Guarantee:** No review submission is created if required information is missing/invalid.
USER - **Related Business Rules:** BR-EL-027, BR-EL-036
USER - **Related Functional Requirements:** CFR-EL-021
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-04
USER 
USER ### EL-UC-011 — Review Resumption
USER 
USER - **Primary Actor:** Applicable Sanctioning Authority
USER - **Supporting Actors / Systems:** Employee; leave/attendance records
USER - **Goal / Description:** Review the submitted resumption and apply only the adjustments required by the actual return date.
USER - **Trigger:** A resumption submission is assigned for review.
USER - **Preconditions:** • A valid resumption submission exists for EL/COL/VL.
USER • Actor is the applicable Sanctioning Authority.
USER - **UC Type:** Actor-triggered
USER 
USER Main Success Scenario
USER 
USER M1. Authority reviews the submitted actual resumption date/details.
USER 
USER M2. System compares actual resumption date with approved end date.
USER 
USER M3. If dates match, Authority records review completion; no approved leave-period or balance adjustment is made.
USER 
USER M4. If actual date is earlier or later, Authority confirms the review outcome and System records actual leave period, adjusts balance accordingly, and synchronizes relevant leave/attendance records.
USER 
USER Alternative / Exception Flows
USER 
USER A1 No generic 'close leave' state transition is asserted for normal resumption in this baseline.
USER 
USER - **Success Postcondition:** Resumption review is recorded; early/late return produces the required data/balance synchronization.
USER - **Failure / Minimal Guarantee:** No balance/period adjustment is applied before required review.
USER - **Related Business Rules:** BR-EL-027–028, BR-EL-036
USER - **Related Functional Requirements:** CFR-EL-021
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** CW-EL-04
USER 
USER ### EL-UC-012 — View Leave Balances and History
USER 
USER - **Primary Actor:** Employee
USER - **Supporting Actors / Systems:** None
USER - **Goal / Description:** Inspect own current leave balances and leave-account/request history, including request status and final decisions.
USER - **Trigger:** Employee wants to inspect current balances or historical/request status information.
USER - **Preconditions:** • Employee is authenticated and authorized to access own leave information.
USER - **UC Type:** Actor-triggered
USER 
USER Main Success Scenario
USER 
USER M1. Employee opens leave balance/history inquiry.
USER 
USER M2. System displays current leave balances.
USER 
USER M3. System displays accessible leave-account/request history including current request status and recorded final decisions.
USER 
USER - **Success Postcondition:** Requested balance/history information is displayed.
USER - **Failure / Minimal Guarantee:** No other employee's protected leave information is exposed.
USER - **Related Business Rules:** BR-EL-036
USER - **Related Functional Requirements:** CFR-EL-016, CFR-EL-022
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** N/A
USER 
USER ### EL-UC-013 — Obtain Printable/Exportable Leave Application
USER 
USER - **Primary Actor:** Employee
USER - **Supporting Actors / Systems:** None
USER - **Goal / Description:** Obtain a printable or exportable representation of an accessible leave application.
USER - **Trigger:** Employee needs a printable/exportable copy of an accessible leave application.
USER - **Preconditions:** • Employee is authorized to access the application.
USER - **UC Type:** Actor-triggered
USER 
USER Main Success Scenario
USER 
USER M1. Employee selects an accessible leave application.
USER 
USER M2. Employee requests print/export representation.
USER 
USER M3. System produces the representation for the selected application.
USER 
USER - **Success Postcondition:** Printable/exportable representation is available.
USER - **Failure / Minimal Guarantee:** No representation is produced for an application the employee is not authorized to access.
USER - **Related Business Rules:** BR-EL-036
USER - **Related Functional Requirements:** CFR-EL-023
USER - **Related Workflow(s):** N/A
USER ```
