USER class ApplyLeaveView(generics.CreateAPIView):
USER     """Create a new leave application."""
USER 
USER     permission_classes = [HasLeaveModuleGrant, HasPermission("leave.application.create")]
USER     serializer_class = ApplyLeaveSerializer
USER 
USER     def create(self, request: Any, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Response:
USER         try:
USER             serializer = self.get_serializer(data=request.data)
USER             serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
USER             instance = apply_leave(
USER                 actor_id=request.principal.user_id,
USER                 category=serializer.validated_data["category"],
USER                 requested_from=serializer.validated_data["requested_from"],
USER                 requested_to=serializer.validated_data["requested_to"],
USER                 purpose=serializer.validated_data["purpose"],
USER                 station_leave_details=serializer.validated_data.get("station_leave_details"),
USER                 nominated_substitute_id=serializer.validated_data.get("nominated_substitute_id"),
USER             )
USER             return Response(LeaveApplicationDetailSerializer(instance).data, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
USER         except ValueError as e:
USER             return Response({"error": str(e)}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)
USER

USER class ExtendLeaveView(generics.UpdateAPIView):
USER     """Request an extension of ongoing leave."""
USER 
USER     permission_classes = [HasLeaveModuleGrant, HasPermission("leave.application.create")]
USER     serializer_class = RequestExtensionSerializer
USER 
USER     def put(self, request: Any, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Response:
USER         try:
USER             application_id = kwargs["pk"]
USER             serializer = self.get_serializer(data=request.data)
USER             serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
USER             
USER             extension_from = serializer.validated_data["extension_from"]
USER             extension_to = serializer.validated_data["extension_to"]
USER             purpose = serializer.validated_data["purpose"]
USER             station_leave_details = serializer.validated_data.get("station_leave_details")
USER             nominated_substitute_id = serializer.validated_data.get("nominated_substitute_id")
USER 
USER             request_extension(
USER                 actor_id=request.principal.user_id,
USER                 application_id=application_id,
USER                 extension_from=extension_from,
USER                 extension_to=extension_to,
USER                 purpose=purpose,
USER                 station_leave_details=station_leave_details,
USER                 nominated_substitute_id=nominated_substitute_id,
USER             )
USER             return Response(status=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT)
USER         except ValueError as e:
USER             return Response({"error": str(e)}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

ASSISTANT """
ASSISTANT Services for Leave Application management.
ASSISTANT Implements business logic for application lifecycle, decisions, and extensions.
ASSISTANT """
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT from typing import Any

ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT # Assuming models are imported from the same module or available context

ASSISTANT from modules.leave.models import LeaveApplication, LeaveActionHistory
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT def withdraw_pending_request(actor_id: int, application_id: int) -> None:
ASSISTANT     """Withdraw a pending leave application."""
ASSISTANT     try:
ASSISTANT         application = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
ASSISTANT         
ASSISTANT         # Check if the application belongs to the actor (optional but good practice)
ASSISTANT         # and if it is in a withdrawable state (e.g., 'pending')
ASSISTANT         if application.user_id != actor_id:
ASSISTANT             raise ValueError("You can only withdraw your own applications.")
ASSISTANT         
ASSISTANT         if application.status not in ['pending', 'draft']:  # Adjust based on actual status enum
ASSISTANT             raise ValueError(f"Cannot withdraw application with status {application.status}.")
ASSISTANT             
ASSISTANT         # Update status to withdrawn
ASSISTANT         application.status = 'withdrawn'
ASSISTANT         application.save()
ASSISTANT         
ASSISTANT         # Log the action
ASSISTANT         history = LeaveActionHistory.objects.create(
ASSISTANT             application_id=application_id,
ASSISTANT             actor_id=actor_id,
ASSISTANT             action_type='withdrawal',
ASSISTANT             description=f"Application withdrawn by user {actor_id}"
ASSISTANT         )
ASSISTANT     except LeaveApplication.DoesNotExist:
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(f"Application with ID {application_id} not found.")
ASSISTANT     except ValidationError as e:
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(str(e))
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT

USER modules/leave/services/applications.py
USER ```
USER """
USER Service layer for leave applications.
USER Implements core business logic: validation, routing, consent handling, and decision recording.
USER Uses Django transactions to ensure atomicity of state changes and history logging.
USER """
USER 
USER from datetime import date
USER from typing import Optional, Tuple
USER 
USER from django.db import transaction, models
USER from django.core.exceptions import ValidationError as DjangoValidationError
USER 
USER from modules.directory.models import UserRef
USER from modules.leave.domain.policy import (
USER     LeaveCategory,
USER     calculate_consumed_days,
USER     check_applicant_overlap,
USER     requires_substitute,
USER     unit_head_decision_required,
USER     get_categories_requiring_unit_head_decision,
USER )
USER from modules.leave.models import (
USER     LeaveApplication,
USER     LeaveBalance,
USER     LeaveActionHistory,
USER     LeaveCategory as ModelLeaveCategory,
USER )
USER from fusion_auth.permissions import HasPermission
USER 
USER 
USER def apply_leave(
USER     actor_id: int,
USER     category: str,
USER     requested_from: date,
USER     requested_to: date,
USER     purpose: str,
USER     station_leave_details: Optional[dict] = None,
USER     nominated_substitute_id: Optional[int] = None,
USER ) -> LeaveApplication:
USER     """
USER     Create and submit a new leave application.
USER     
USER     Validates eligibility, balance, overlaps, and substitutes.
USER     Routes to substitute consent if required, otherwise proceeds to Unit Head review.
USER     
USER     Args:
USER         actor_id: ID of the employee submitting the request.
USER         category: Leave category (e.g., 'CL', 'RH').
USER         requested_from: Start date of leave.
USER         requested_to: End date of leave.
USER         purpose: Reason for leave.
USER         station_leave_details: Optional JSON details for station leave.
USER         nominated_substitute_id: ID of the nominated substitute (if applicable).
USER     
USER     Returns:
USER         The created LeaveApplication instance.
USER     
USER     Raises:
USER         ValueError: If validation fails or action is not allowed in current status.
USER         DjangoValidationError: If domain policy rules are violated.
USER     """
USER     # Map string category to Enum
USER     try:
USER         category_enum = ModelLeaveCategory(category)
USER     except ValueError:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid leave category: {category}")
USER 
USER     application, created = LeaveApplication.objects.get_or_create(
USER         user_id=actor_id,
USER         defaults={
USER             'category': category_enum,
USER             'requested_from': requested_from,
USER             'requested_to': requested_to,
USER             'purpose': purpose,
USER             'station_leave_details': station_leave_details,
USER             'nominated_substitute_id': nominated_substitute_id,
USER             'status': 'draft',
USER         },
USER     )
USER 
USER     if not created:
USER         # Check if status allows modification (only 'draft' or 'submitted' before full processing)
USER         # However, per BR-EL-034, in-place modification is blocked. 
USER         # We assume this function is called only for new applications.
USER         # If an existing draft exists, we might need to handle it differently, 
USER         # but the spec implies a fresh application flow.
USER         pass
USER 
USER     # Domain Validation
USER     try:
USER         policy = get_policy_config(category_enum)
USER         
USER         # Check balance (simplified: assume sufficient balance if created or updated by service)
USER         # In a full system, we would query LeaveBalance for the user/year/category.
USER         # For this service layer, we assume the balance check is handled or passed.
USER         # We will perform a placeholder check to ensure logic exists.
USER         _check_balance(actor_id, category_enum, requested_from, requested_to)
USER 
USER         # Check overlap (simplified: assume no active leave for actor)
USER         _check_overlap(actor_id, requested_from, requested_to)
USER 
USER         # Check substitute eligibility if required
USER         if requires_substitute(category_enum):
USER             if nominated_substitute_id is None:
USER                 raise ValueError("Substitute must be nominated for this category.")
USER             _validate_substitute_eligibility(nominated_substitute_id, actor_id, requested_from, requested_to)
USER     except (ValueError, DjangoValidationError) as e:
USER         # Log history entry for validation failure before raising
USER         _log_action_history(
USER             application=application,
USER             actor_id=actor_id,
USER             action_type="validation_failed",
USER             description=str(e),
USER         )
USER         raise
USER 
USER     # Create the application instance (if not already created above)
USER     if not created:
USER         application.save()
USER 
USER     # Determine routing path
USER     if requires_substitute(category_enum):
USER         # Route to substitute consent
USER         status = 'submitted'
USER         _log_action_history(
USER             application=application,
USER             actor_id=actor_id,
USER             action_type="substitute_consent_required",
USER             description=f"Application submitted. Awaiting substitute consent.",
USER         )
USER     else:
USER         # Route to Unit Head review (or Director self-sanction if applicable)
USER         status = 'submitted'
USER         _log_action_history(
USER             application=application,
USER             actor_id=actor_id,
USER             action_type="submitted_for_review",
USER             description=f"Application submitted for {category_enum.value} leave.",
USER         )
USER 
USER     # Update status to submitted
USER     application.status = status
USER     application.save()
USER 
USER     return application
USER 
USER 
USER def respond_to_substitute_request(
USER     actor_id: int,
USER     application_id: int,
USER     accept: bool,
USER ) -> None:
USER     """
USER     Accept or decline a substitute request.
USER     
USER     Args:
USER         actor_id: ID of the substitute (or responsibility holder).
USER         application_id: ID of the associated leave application.
USER         accept: True to accept, False to decline.
USER     
USER     Raises:
USER         ValueError: If action is not allowed in current status.
USER     """
USER     application = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     if application.status not in ['submitted', 'under_review']:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Cannot respond to substitute request for application in status {application.status}.")
USER 
USER     # Update application status based on response
USER     if accept:
USER         new_status = 'under_review'
USER         description = "Substitute consent accepted."
USER     else:
USER         new_status = 'submitted'  # Reverts to applicant action required state effectively
USER         description = "Substitute consent declined."
USER 
USER     application.status = new_status
USER     application.save()
USER 
USER     _log_action_history(
USER         application=application,
USER         actor_id=actor_id,
USER         action_type="substitute_response",
USER         description=description,
USER     )
USER 
USER 
USER def nominate_replacement_substitute(
USER     actor_id: int,
USER     application_id: int,
USER     new_substitute_id: int,
USER ) -> None:
USER     """
USER     Nominate a replacement substitute after a decline.
USER     
USER     Args:
USER         actor_id: ID of the employee (applicant).
USER         application_id: ID of the associated leave application.
USER         new_substitute_id: ID of the new nominated substitute.
USER     
USER     Raises:
USER         ValueError: If action is not allowed in current status.
USER     """
USER     application = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     # Check if we are in a state where replacement is needed (e.g., after decline)
USER     # Per EL-UC-019, this happens when substitute declines and request enters 'Applicant Action Required'.
USER     # We assume status reflects this or is 'submitted' awaiting another response.
USER     if application.status not in ['submitted', 'under_review']:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Cannot nominate replacement substitute for application in status {application.status}.")
USER 
USER     # Validate new substitute eligibility (simplified)
USER     _validate_substitute_eligibility(new_substitute_id, actor_id, application.requested_from, application.requested_to)
USER 
USER     # Update the nominated substitute ID
USER     application.nominated_substitute_id = new_substitute_id
USER     application.save()
USER 
USER     _log_action_history(
USER         application=application,
USER         actor_id=actor_id,
USER         action_type="substitute_renomination",
USER         description=f"New substitute {new_substitute_id} nominated.",
USER     )
USER 
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def unit_head_review(
USER     actor_id: int,
USER     application_id: int,
USER     decision: str,
USER ) -> None:
USER     """
USER     Record Unit Head's review decision.
USER     
USER     For CL/RH: Approve or Reject.
USER     For SCL/EL/COL/VL: Recommended or Not Recommended.
USER     
USER     Args:
USER         actor_id: ID of the Unit Head.
USER         application_id: ID of the leave application.
USER         decision: 'approve', 'reject', 'recommended', or 'not_recommended'.
USER     
USER     Raises:
USER         ValueError: If action is not allowed in current status.
USER     """
USER     application = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     # Determine expected statuses based on category and decision type
USER     category_enum = ModelLeaveCategory(application.category)
USER     
USER     if unit_head_decision_required(category_enum):
USER         # CL/RH: Unit Head makes final decision
USER         allowed_statuses = ['submitted', 'under_review']
USER         valid_decisions = ['approve', 'reject']
USER     else:
USER         # SCL/EL/COL/VL: Unit Head recommends
USER         allowed_statuses = ['submitted', 'under_review']
USER         valid_decisions = ['recommended', 'not_recommended']
USER 
USER     if application.status not in allowed_statuses:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Unit Head can only review applications in status {allowed_statuses}. Current status: {application.status}")
USER 
USER     if decision not in valid_decisions:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid decision '{decision}' for category {category_enum.value}.")
USER 
USER     # Update application status and record history
USER     if decision == 'approve':
USER         new_status = 'approved'
USER         description = f"Unit Head approved {application.category.value} leave."
USER     elif decision == 'reject':
USER         new_status = 'rejected'
USER         description = f"Unit Head rejected {application.category.value} leave."
USER     elif decision == 'recommended':
USER         new_status = 'recommended'
USER         description = f"Unit Head recommended {application.category.value} leave for higher sanction."
USER     else:  # not_recommended
USER         new_status = 'not_recommended'
USER         description = f"Unit Head not recommended {application.category.value} leave for higher sanction."
USER 
USER     application.status = new_status
USER     application.save()
USER 
USER     _log_action_history(
USER         application=application,
USER         actor_id=actor_id,
USER         action_type="unit_head_decision",
USER         description=description,
USER     )
USER 
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def establishment_verify_and_route(
USER     actor_id: int,
USER     application_id: int,
USER ) -> None:
USER     """
USER     Perform Establishment-level verification and route to final Sanctioning Authority.
USER     
USER     This is a placeholder for the logic defined in BR-EL-018 and EL-UC-004.
USER     In this implementation, it assumes a simple routing step where the application
USER     is verified (e.g., completeness check) and then routed based on configuration.
USER     
USER     Args:
USER         actor_id: ID of the Establishment Processing Role (e.g., Deputy Registrar).
USER         application_id: ID of the leave application.
USER     
USER     Raises:
USER         ValueError: If action is not allowed in current status.
USER     """
USER     application = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     # Check if this is a higher-sanction case requiring routing
USER     category_enum = ModelLeaveCategory(application.category)
USER     if unit_head_decision_required(category_enum):
USER         raise ValueError(f"Unit Head decision required for {category_enum.value}, no establishment routing needed.")
USER 
USER     allowed_statuses = ['recommended', 'not_recommended']
USER     if application.status not in allowed_statuses:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Establishment can only process applications in status {allowed_statuses}. Current status: {application.status}")
USER 
USER     # Placeholder verification logic (e.g., check for required fields)
USER     # In a real system, this would involve complex hierarchy configuration checks.
USER     _verify_establishment_requirements(application)
USER 
USER     # Route to final Sanctioning Authority (simulated by updating status)
USER     # The actual routing might involve creating a work item or changing status directly.
USER     # Per BR-EL-019, we resolve the competent authority. Here, we assume the next step is final sanction.
USER     new_status = 'under_review'  # Or a specific 'awaiting_final_sanction' if defined
USER     description = f"Establishment verified and routed to final Sanctioning Authority."
USER 
USER     application.status = new_status
USER     application.save()
USER 
USER     _log_action_history(
USER         application=application,
USER         actor_id=actor_id,
USER         action_type="establishment_routing",
USER         description=description,
USER     )
USER 
USER 
USER @transaction.atomic
USER def final_sanction_decision(
USER     actor_id: int,
USER     application_id: int,
USER     decision: str,
USER ) -> None:
USER     """
USER     Record the final sanctioning decision.
USER     
USER     Approve or Reject. Updates balance on approval.
USER     
USER     Args:
USER         actor_id: ID of the Sanctioning Authority (e.g., Dean, Registrar, Director).
USER         application_id: ID of the leave application.
USER         decision: 'approve' or 'reject'.
USER     
USER     Raises:
USER         ValueError: If action is not allowed in current status.
USER     """
USER     application = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
USER 
USER     # Check if this is a final sanction case (not Unit Head decision)
USER     category_enum = ModelLeaveCategory(application.category)
USER     if unit_head_decision_required(category_enum):
USER         raise ValueError(f"Unit Head makes the final decision for {category_enum.value}, no higher authority needed.")
USER 
USER     allowed_statuses = ['recommended', 'not_recommended']
USER     if application.status not in allowed_statuses:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Final Sanctioning Authority can only process applications in status {allowed_statuses}. Current status: {application.status}")
USER 
USER     if decision not in ['approve', 'reject']:
USER         raise ValueError(f"Invalid final decision '{decision}'. Must be 'approve' or 'reject'.")
USER 
USER     # Update application status
USER     new_status = 'approved' if decision == 'approve' else 'rejected'
USER     description = f"Final Sanctioning Authority {decision}ed {application.category.value} leave."
USER 
USER     application.status = new_status
USER     application.save()
USER 
USER     # Handle balance update on approval (BR-EL-025, BR-EL-023)
USER     if decision == 'approve':
USER         _update_balance_on_approval(application, actor_id)
USER 
USER     _log_action_history(
USER         application=application,
USER         actor_id=actor_id,
USER         action_type="final_sanction",
USER         description=description,
USER     )
USER

USER def _update_balance_on_approval(application: LeaveApplication, actor_id: int) -> None:

USER config/urls.py
USER ```
USER """Root URLs.
USER 
USER Every module mounts itself under /api/v1/<module>/. A module that is not in
USER INSTALLED_APPS contributes no URLs at all — that is what "independent" means
USER here: removing a module is deleting a directory and one settings line.
USER """
USER from django.urls import include, path
USER from drf_spectacular.views import SpectacularAPIView, SpectacularSwaggerView
USER 
USER import core.api.schema  # noqa: F401  registers the auth schemes by import
USER from core.api.health import healthz, readyz
USER 
USER urlpatterns = [
USER     path("healthz", healthz, name="healthz"),
USER     path("readyz", readyz, name="readyz"),
USER     path("api/v1/", include("fusion_auth.urls")),

USER class ApplyLeaveView(generics.CreateAPIView):
USER     """Create a new leave application."""
USER 
USER     permission_classes = [HasLeaveModuleGrant, HasPermission("leave.application.create")]
USER     serializer_class = ApplyLeaveSerializer
USER 
USER     def create(self, request: Any, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Response:
USER         try:
USER             serializer = self.get_serializer(data=request.data)
USER             serializer.is_valid(raise_exception=True)
USER             instance = apply_leave(
USER                 actor_id=request.principal.user_id,
USER                 category=serializer.validated_data["category"],
USER                 requested_from=serializer.validated_data["requested_from"],
USER                 requested_to=serializer.validated_data["requested_to"],
USER                 purpose=serializer.validated_data["purpose"],
USER                 station_leave_details=serializer.validated_data.get("station_leave_details"),
USER                 nominated_substitute_id=serializer.validated_data.get("nominated_substitute_id"),
USER             )
USER             return Response(LeaveApplicationDetailSerializer(instance).data, status=status.HTTP_201_CREATED)
USER         except ValueError as e:
USER             return Response({"error": str(e)}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

USER

USER     serializer_class = LeaveBalanceSerializer
USER 
USER     def get(self, request: Any, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Response:
USER         try:
USER             year = kwargs.get("year")
USER             if not year:
USER                 return Response({"error": "Year parameter is required"}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)
USER 
USER             balances = get_balances(
USER                 user_id=request.principal.user_id,
USER                 year=year,
USER             )
USER             return Response(balances)
USER         except ValueError as e:
USER             return Response({"error": str(e)}, status=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST)

ASSISTANT def withdraw_pending_request(actor_id: int, application_id: int) -> None:
ASSISTANT     """Withdraw a pending leave application."""
ASSISTANT     try:
ASSISTANT         application = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
ASSISTANT         
ASSISTANT         # Check if the application belongs to the actor (optional but good practice)
ASSISTANT         # and if it is in a withdrawable state (e.g., 'pending')
ASSISTANT         if application.user_id != actor_id:
ASSISTANT             raise ValueError("You can only withdraw your own applications.")
ASSISTANT         
ASSISTANT         if application.status not in ['pending', 'draft']:  # Adjust based on actual status enum
ASSISTANT             raise ValueError(f"Cannot withdraw application with status {application.status}.")
ASSISTANT             
ASSISTANT         # Update status to withdrawn
ASSISTANT         application.status = 'withdrawn'
ASSISTANT         application.save()
ASSISTANT         
ASSISTANT         # Log the action
ASSISTANT         history = LeaveActionHistory.objects.create(
ASSISTANT             application_id=application_id,
ASSISTANT             actor_id=actor_id,
ASSISTANT             action_type='withdrawal',
ASSISTANT             description=f"Application withdrawn by user {actor_id}"
ASSISTANT         )
ASSISTANT     except LeaveApplication.DoesNotExist:
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(f"Application with ID {application_id} not found.")
ASSISTANT     except ValidationError as e:
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(str(e))

ASSISTANT     actor_id: int,
ASSISTANT     category: str,
ASSISTANT     requested_from: date,
ASSISTANT     requested_to: date,
ASSISTANT     purpose: str,
ASSISTANT     station_leave_details: Optional[dict] = None,
ASSISTANT     nominated_substitute_id: Optional[int] = None,
ASSISTANT ) -> LeaveApplication:
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     Create and submit a new leave application.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Validates eligibility, balance, overlaps, and substitutes.
ASSISTANT     Routes to substitute consent if required, otherwise proceeds to Unit Head review.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Args:
ASSISTANT         actor_id: ID of the employee submitting the request.
ASSISTANT         category: Leave category (e.g., 'CL', 'RH').
ASSISTANT         requested_from: Start date of leave.
ASSISTANT         requested_to: End date of leave.
ASSISTANT         purpose: Reason for leave.
ASSISTANT         station_leave_details: Optional JSON details for station leave.
ASSISTANT         nominated_substitute_id: ID of the nominated substitute (if applicable).
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Returns:
ASSISTANT         The created LeaveApplication instance.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Raises:
ASSISTANT         ValueError: If validation fails or action is not allowed in current status.
ASSISTANT         DjangoValidationError: If domain policy rules are violated.
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     # Map string category to Enum
ASSISTANT     try:
ASSISTANT         category_enum = ModelLeaveCategory(category)
ASSISTANT     except ValueError:
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(f"Invalid leave category: {category}")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     application, created = LeaveApplication.objects.get_or_create(
ASSISTANT         user_id=actor_id,
ASSISTANT         defaults={
ASSISTANT             'category': category_enum,
ASSISTANT             'requested_from': requested_from,
ASSISTANT             'requested_to': requested_to,
ASSISTANT             'purpose': purpose,
ASSISTANT             'station_leave_details': station_leave_details,
ASSISTANT             'nominated_substitute_id': nominated_substitute_id,
ASSISTANT             'status': 'draft',
ASSISTANT         },
ASSISTANT     )
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     if not created:
ASSISTANT         # Check if status allows modification (only 'draft' or 'submitted' before full processing)
ASSISTANT         # However, per BR-EL-034, in-place modification is blocked. 
ASSISTANT         # We assume this function is called only for new applications.
ASSISTANT         # If an existing draft exists, we might need to handle it differently, 
ASSISTANT         # but the spec implies a fresh application flow.
ASSISTANT         pass
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Domain Validation
ASSISTANT     try:
ASSISTANT         policy = get_policy_config(category_enum)
ASSISTANT         
ASSISTANT         # Check balance (simplified: assume sufficient balance if created or updated by service)
ASSISTANT         # In a full system, we would query LeaveBalance for the user/year/category.
ASSISTANT         # For this service layer, we assume the balance check is handled or passed.
ASSISTANT         # We will perform a placeholder check to ensure logic exists.
ASSISTANT         _check_balance(actor_id, category_enum, requested_from, requested_to)
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT         # Check overlap (simplified: assume no active leave for actor)
ASSISTANT         _check_overlap(actor_id, requested_from, requested_to)
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT         # Check substitute eligibility if required
ASSISTANT         if requires_substitute(category_enum):
ASSISTANT             if nominated_substitute_id is None:
ASSISTANT                 raise ValueError("Substitute must be nominated for this category.")
ASSISTANT             _validate_substitute_eligibility(nominated_substitute_id, actor_id, requested_from, requested_to)
ASSISTANT     except (ValueError, DjangoValidationError) as e:
ASSISTANT         # Log history entry for validation failure before raising
ASSISTANT         _log_action_history(
ASSISTANT             application=application,
ASSISTANT             actor_id=actor_id,
ASSISTANT             action_type="validation_failed",
ASSISTANT             description=str(e),
ASSISTANT         )
ASSISTANT         raise
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Create the application instance (if not already created above)
ASSISTANT     if not created:
ASSISTANT         application.save()
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Determine routing path
ASSISTANT     if requires_substitute(category_enum):
ASSISTANT         # Route to substitute consent
ASSISTANT         status = 'submitted'
ASSISTANT         _log_action_history(
ASSISTANT             application=application,
ASSISTANT             actor_id=actor_id,
ASSISTANT             action_type="substitute_consent_required",
ASSISTANT             description=f"Application submitted. Awaiting substitute consent.",
ASSISTANT         )
ASSISTANT     else:
ASSISTANT         # Route to Unit Head review (or Director self-sanction if applicable)
ASSISTANT         status = 'submitted'
ASSISTANT         _log_action_history(
ASSISTANT             application=application,
ASSISTANT             actor_id=actor_id,
ASSISTANT             action_type="submitted_for_review",
ASSISTANT             description=f"Application submitted for {category_enum.value} leave.",
ASSISTANT         )
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Update status to submitted
ASSISTANT     application.status = status
ASSISTANT     application.save()
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     return application
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT def respond_to_substitute_request(
ASSISTANT     actor_id: int,
ASSISTANT     application_id: int,
ASSISTANT     accept: bool,
ASSISTANT ) -> None:
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     Accept or decline a substitute request.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Args:
ASSISTANT         actor_id: ID of the substitute (or responsibility holder).
ASSISTANT         application_id: ID of the associated leave application.
ASSISTANT         accept: True to accept, False to decline.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Raises:
ASSISTANT         ValueError: If action is not allowed in current status.
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     application = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     if application.status not in ['submitted', 'under_review']:
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(f"Cannot respond to substitute request for application in status {application.status}.")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Update application status based on response
ASSISTANT     if accept:
ASSISTANT         new_status = 'under_review'
ASSISTANT         description = "Substitute consent accepted."
ASSISTANT     else:
ASSISTANT         new_status = 'submitted'  # Reverts to applicant action required state effectively
ASSISTANT         description = "Substitute consent declined."
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     application.status = new_status
ASSISTANT     application.save()
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     _log_action_history(
ASSISTANT         application=application,
ASSISTANT         actor_id=actor_id,
ASSISTANT         action_type="substitute_response",
ASSISTANT         description=description,
ASSISTANT     )
ASSISTANT

ASSISTANT def nominate_replacement_substitute(

ASSISTANT     application_id: int,
ASSISTANT     new_substitute_id: int,
ASSISTANT ) -> None:
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     Nominate a replacement substitute after a decline.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Args:
ASSISTANT         actor_id: ID of the employee (applicant).
ASSISTANT         application_id: ID of the associated leave application.
ASSISTANT         new_substitute_id: ID of the new nominated substitute.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Raises:
ASSISTANT         ValueError: If action is not allowed in current status.
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     application = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Check if we are in a state where replacement is needed (e.g., after decline)
ASSISTANT     # Per EL-UC-019, this happens when substitute declines and request enters 'Applicant Action Required'.
ASSISTANT     # We assume status reflects this or is 'submitted' awaiting another response.
ASSISTANT     if application.status not in ['submitted', 'under_review']:
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(f"Cannot nominate replacement substitute for application in status {application.status}.")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Validate new substitute eligibility (simplified)
ASSISTANT     _validate_substitute_eligibility(new_substitute_id, actor_id, application.requested_from, application.requested_to)
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Update the nominated substitute ID
ASSISTANT     application.nominated_substitute_id = new_substitute_id
ASSISTANT     application.save()
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     _log_action_history(
ASSISTANT         application=application,
ASSISTANT         actor_id=actor_id,
ASSISTANT         action_type="substitute_renomination",
ASSISTANT         description=f"New substitute {new_substitute_id} nominated.",
ASSISTANT     )

ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT @transaction.atomic
ASSISTANT def unit_head_review(
ASSISTANT     actor_id: int,
ASSISTANT     application_id: int,
ASSISTANT     decision: str,
ASSISTANT ) -> None:
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     Record Unit Head's review decision.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     For CL/RH: Approve or Reject.
ASSISTANT     For SCL/EL/COL/VL: Recommended or Not Recommended.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Args:
ASSISTANT         actor_id: ID of the Unit Head.
ASSISTANT         application_id: ID of the leave application.
ASSISTANT         decision: 'approve', 'reject', 'recommended', or 'not_recommended'.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Raises:
ASSISTANT         ValueError: If action is not allowed in current status.
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     application = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Determine expected statuses based on category and decision type
ASSISTANT     category_enum = ModelLeaveCategory(application.category)
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     if unit_head_decision_required(category_enum):
ASSISTANT         # CL/RH: Unit Head makes final decision
ASSISTANT         allowed_statuses = ['submitted', 'under_review']
ASSISTANT         valid_decisions = ['approve', 'reject']
ASSISTANT     else:
ASSISTANT         # SCL/EL/COL/VL: Unit Head recommends
ASSISTANT         allowed_statuses = ['submitted', 'under_review']
ASSISTANT         valid_decisions = ['recommended', 'not_recommended']
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     if application.status not in allowed_statuses:
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(f"Unit Head can only review applications in status {allowed_statuses}. Current status: {application.status}")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     if decision not in valid_decisions:
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(f"Invalid decision '{decision}' for category {category_enum.value}.")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Update application status and record history
ASSISTANT     if decision == 'approve':
ASSISTANT         new_status = 'approved'
ASSISTANT         description = f"Unit Head approved {application.category.value} leave."
ASSISTANT     elif decision == 'reject':
ASSISTANT         new_status = 'rejected'
ASSISTANT         description = f"Unit Head rejected {application.category.value} leave."
ASSISTANT     elif decision == 'recommended':
ASSISTANT         new_status = 'recommended'
ASSISTANT         description = f"Unit Head recommended {application.category.value} leave for higher sanction."
ASSISTANT     else:  # not_recommended
ASSISTANT         new_status = 'not_recommended'
ASSISTANT         description = f"Unit Head not recommended {application.category.value} leave for higher sanction."
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     application.status = new_status
ASSISTANT     application.save()
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     _log_action_history(
ASSISTANT         application=application,
ASSISTANT         actor_id=actor_id,
ASSISTANT         action_type="unit_head_decision",
ASSISTANT         description=description,
ASSISTANT     )
ASSISTANT

ASSISTANT @transaction.atomic
ASSISTANT def establishment_verify_and_route(
ASSISTANT     actor_id: int,
ASSISTANT     application_id: int,
ASSISTANT ) -> None:
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     Perform Establishment-level verification and route to final Sanctioning Authority.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     This is a placeholder for the logic defined in BR-EL-018 and EL-UC-004.
ASSISTANT     In this implementation, it assumes a simple routing step where the application
ASSISTANT     is verified (e.g., completeness check) and then routed based on configuration.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Args:
ASSISTANT         actor_id: ID of the Establishment Processing Role (e.g., Deputy Registrar).
ASSISTANT         application_id: ID of the leave application.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Raises:
ASSISTANT         ValueError: If action is not allowed in current status.
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     application = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Check if this is a higher-sanction case requiring routing
ASSISTANT     category_enum = ModelLeaveCategory(application.category)
ASSISTANT     if unit_head_decision_required(category_enum):
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(f"Unit Head decision required for {category_enum.value}, no establishment routing needed.")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     allowed_statuses = ['recommended', 'not_recommended']
ASSISTANT     if application.status not in allowed_statuses:
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(f"Establishment can only process applications in status {allowed_statuses}. Current status: {application.status}")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Placeholder verification logic (e.g., check for required fields)
ASSISTANT     # In a real system, this would involve complex hierarchy configuration checks.
ASSISTANT     _verify_establishment_requirements(application)
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Route to final Sanctioning Authority (simulated by updating status)
ASSISTANT     # The actual routing might involve creating a work item or changing status directly.
ASSISTANT     # Per BR-EL-019, we resolve the competent authority. Here, we assume the next step is final sanction.
ASSISTANT     new_status = 'under_review'  # Or a specific 'awaiting_final_sanction' if defined
ASSISTANT     description = f"Establishment verified and routed to final Sanctioning Authority."
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     application.status = new_status
ASSISTANT     application.save()
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     _log_action_history(
ASSISTANT         application=application,
ASSISTANT         actor_id=actor_id,
ASSISTANT         action_type="establishment_routing",
ASSISTANT         description=description,
ASSISTANT     )
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT @transaction.atomic
ASSISTANT def final_sanction_decision(
ASSISTANT     actor_id: int,
ASSISTANT     application_id: int,
ASSISTANT     decision: str,
ASSISTANT ) -> None:
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     Record the final sanctioning decision.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Approve or Reject. Updates balance on approval.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Args:
ASSISTANT         actor_id: ID of the Sanctioning Authority (e.g., Dean, Registrar, Director).
ASSISTANT         application_id: ID of the leave application.
ASSISTANT         decision: 'approve' or 'reject'.
ASSISTANT     
ASSISTANT     Raises:
ASSISTANT         ValueError: If action is not allowed in current status.
ASSISTANT     """
ASSISTANT     application = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Check if this is a final sanction case (not Unit Head decision)
ASSISTANT     category_enum = ModelLeaveCategory(application.category)
ASSISTANT     if unit_head_decision_required(category_enum):
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(f"Unit Head makes the final decision for {category_enum.value}, no higher authority needed.")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     allowed_statuses = ['recommended', 'not_recommended']
ASSISTANT     if application.status not in allowed_statuses:
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(f"Final Sanctioning Authority can only process applications in status {allowed_statuses}. Current status: {application.status}")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     if decision not in ['approve', 'reject']:
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(f"Invalid final decision '{decision}'. Must be 'approve' or 'reject'.")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Update application status
ASSISTANT     new_status = 'approved' if decision == 'approve' else 'rejected'
ASSISTANT     description = f"Final Sanctioning Authority {decision}ed {application.category.value} leave."
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     application.status = new_status
ASSISTANT     application.save()
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     # Handle balance update on approval (BR-EL-025, BR-EL-023)
ASSISTANT     if decision == 'approve':
ASSISTANT         _update_balance_on_approval(application, actor_id)
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT     _log_action_history(
ASSISTANT         application=application,
ASSISTANT         actor_id=actor_id,
ASSISTANT         action_type="final_sanction",
ASSISTANT         description=description,
ASSISTANT     )
ASSISTANT

ASSISTANT def withdraw_pending_request(actor_id: int, application_id: int) -> None:
ASSISTANT     """Withdraw a pending leave application."""
ASSISTANT     try:
ASSISTANT         application = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
ASSISTANT         
ASSISTANT         # Check if the application belongs to the actor (optional but good practice)
ASSISTANT         # and if it is in a withdrawable state (e.g., 'pending')
ASSISTANT         if application.user_id != actor_id:
ASSISTANT             raise ValueError("You can only withdraw your own applications.")
ASSISTANT         
ASSISTANT         if application.status not in ['pending', 'draft']:  # Adjust based on actual status enum
ASSISTANT             raise ValueError(f"Cannot withdraw application with status {application.status}.")
ASSISTANT             
ASSISTANT         # Update status to withdrawn
ASSISTANT         application.status = 'withdrawn'
ASSISTANT         application.save()
ASSISTANT         
ASSISTANT         # Log the action
ASSISTANT         history = LeaveActionHistory.objects.create(
ASSISTANT             application_id=application_id,
ASSISTANT             actor_id=actor_id,
ASSISTANT             action_type='withdrawal',
ASSISTANT             description=f"Application withdrawn by user {actor_id}"
ASSISTANT         )
ASSISTANT     except LeaveApplication.DoesNotExist:
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(f"Application with ID {application_id} not found.")
ASSISTANT     except ValidationError as e:
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(str(e))
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT def request_cancellation(actor_id: int, application_id: int) -> None:
ASSISTANT     """Request cancellation of an approved leave."""
ASSISTANT     try:
ASSISTANT         application = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
ASSISTANT         
ASSISTANT         if application.user_id != actor_id:
ASSISTANT             raise ValueError("You can only cancel your own applications.")
ASSISTANT             
ASSISTANT         # Update status to 'cancellation_requested' or similar
ASSISTANT         application.status = 'cancellation_requested'
ASSISTANT         application.save()
ASSISTANT         
ASSISTANT         history = LeaveActionHistory.objects.create(
ASSISTANT             application_id=application_id,
ASSISTANT             actor_id=actor_id,
ASSISTANT             action_type='cancellation_request',
ASSISTANT             description=f"Cancellation requested by user {actor_id}"
ASSISTANT         )
ASSISTANT     except LeaveApplication.DoesNotExist:
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(f"Application with ID {application_id} not found.")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT def decide_cancellation(actor_id: int, application_id: int, decision: str) -> None:
ASSISTANT     """Decide on a cancellation request."""
ASSISTANT     try:
ASSISTANT         application = LeaveApplication.objects.get(id=application_id)
ASSISTANT         
ASSISTANT         if application.status != 'cancellation_requested':
ASSISTANT             raise ValueError(f"Cannot decide cancellation for application with status {application.status}.")
ASSISTANT             
ASSISTANT         if decision == 'approve':
ASSISTANT             # Restore balance and mark as cancelled
ASSISTANT             _update_balance_on_cancellation(application, actor_id)
ASSISTANT             application.status = 'cancelled'
ASSISTANT             description = f"Cancellation approved by {actor_id}."
ASSISTANT         else:  # reject
ASSISTANT             application.status = 'approved'  # Revert to original status or keep pending? Usually revert.
ASSISTANT             description = f"Cancellation rejected by {actor_id}."
ASSISTANT             
ASSISTANT         application.save()
ASSISTANT         
ASSISTANT         history = LeaveActionHistory.objects.create(
ASSISTANT             application_id=application_id,
ASSISTANT             actor_id=actor_id,
ASSISTANT             action_type='cancellation_decision',
ASSISTANT             description=description
ASSISTANT         )
ASSISTANT     except LeaveApplication.DoesNotExist:
ASSISTANT         raise ValueError(f"Application with ID {application_id} not found.")
ASSISTANT 
ASSISTANT

USER │export function errorMessage(e: unknown): string {
USER │  const env = (e as { response?: { data?: { error?: {
USER │    message?: string; details?: { message?: string }[] } } } })
USER │    ?.response?.data?.error;
USER │  if (!env) return "Something went wrong. Please try again.";
USER │  if (env.details?.length) {
USER │    return env.details.map((d) => d.message).filter(Boolean).join(" ") ||
USER │      env.message || "Request failed.";
USER │  }
USER │  return env.message ?? "Request failed.";
USER ⋮
USER │export async function readBlobError(e: unknown): Promise<unknown> {
USER │  const res = (e as { response?: { data?: unknown } })?.response;
USER │  if (res?.data instanceof Blob) {
USER │    try {
USER │      res.data = JSON.parse(await res.data.text());
USER │    } catch {
USER │      res.data = undefined;             // an HTML page, not our envelope
USER │    }
USER │  }
USER │  return e;

USER

USER import type { LeaveApplication } from "../api/types";
USER 
USER type LeaveCategory = "sick" | "personal" | "counseling" | "station";
USER 
USER export default function ApplyLeavePage() {
USER   const submitMutation = useSubmitApplication();
USER   const [formData, setFormData] = useState<Partial<LeaveApplication>>({
USER     leave_type: "sick",
USER     start_date: "",
USER     end_date: "",
USER     reason: "",
USER     unit_head_recommendation: "",
USER     final_sanction_decision: "",
USER   });
USER 
USER   const [category, setCategory] = useState<LeaveCategory>("sick");
USER   const [stationLeaveDetails, setStationLeaveDetails] = useState({
USER     is_station_leave: false,
USER     substitute_user_id: "",
USER   });
USER 
USER   useEffect(() => {
USER     if (category === "station") {
USER       setStationLeaveDetails((prev) => ({ ...prev, is_station_leave: true }));
USER     } else {
USER       setStationLeaveDetails((prev) => ({ ...prev, is_station_leave: false }));
USER     }
USER   }, [category]);
USER 
USER   function handleSubmit() {
USER     if (!formData.start_date || !formData.end_date || !formData.reason) {
USER       notifications.show({
USER         color: "red",
USER         title: "Missing Information",
USER         message: "Please fill in all required fields.",
USER       });
USER       return;
USER     }
USER 
USER     submitMutation.mutate(formData, {
USER       onSuccess: () => {
USER         notifications.show({
USER           color: "blue",
USER           title: "Application Submitted",
USER           message: "Your leave application has been submitted successfully.",
USER         });
USER       },
USER       onError: (e) => {
USER         notifications.show({
USER           color: "red",
USER           title: "Submission Failed",
USER           message: errorMessage(e),
USER         });
USER       },
USER     });
USER   }
USER 
USER   if (submitMutation.isPending) {
USER     return (
USER       <Container size="xl">
USER         <PageHeader title="Apply for Leave" subtitle="Submit a new leave request" />
USER         <Card padding="lg">
USER           <Text c="dimmed" size="sm">Submitting...</Text>
USER         </Card>
USER       </Container>
USER     );
USER   }
USER 
USER   return (
USER     <Container size="xl">
USER       <PageHeader title="Apply for Leave" subtitle="Submit a new leave request" />
USER       <Card padding="lg">
USER         <Group mb="md">
USER           <Select
USER             label="Leave Category"
USER             value={category}
USER             onChange={(value) => setCategory(value as LeaveCategory)}
USER             data={[
USER               { value: "sick", label: "Sick Leave" },
USER               { value: "personal", label: "Personal Leave" },
USER               { value: "counseling", label: "Counseling Leave" },
USER               { value: "station", label: "Station Leave" },
USER             ]}
USER           />
USER         </Group>
USER

USER           label="Start Date"
USER           type="date"
USER           value={formData.start_date || ""}

USER           required
USER         />
